iḵtu, eḻuttukkaṭku moḻikkaṇ māttirai kāraṇamākap piṟappatōr aiyam tīrttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻippaṭuttu icaippiṉum - moḻikkaṇ paṭuttuc colliṉum, terintu vēṟu icaippiṉum - terintukoṇṭu vēṟē colliṉum, eḻuttu iyal tiriyā eṉmaṉār pulavar - uyirum meyyumākiya eḻuttukkaḷ (perukkam curukkam uṭaiyaṉapōṉṟu icaippiṉum,) tattam māttirai iyalpiṉ tiriyā eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aḵkal, a eṉavum, āl, ā eṉavum, kaṭal, ka eṉavum, kāl, kā eṉavum kaṇṭukoḷka.
'vēṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, eṭuttal paṭuttal mutaliya ōcai vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum eḻuttiyal tiriyā veṉpatu koḷka.	(20)
pōliyeḻuttu icaikkumāṟu
54.	akara ikaram aikāram ākum.
itu, pōli eḻuttu āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ikaram - akaramum ikaramum kūṭṭic colla, aikāramākum - aikārampōla ākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	 aiyar, aiyar eṉavarum. atu koḷḷaṟka.	(21)
55.	akara ukaram aukāram ākum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akaram ukaram - akaramum ukaramum kūṭṭic colla, aukāram ākum - aukārampōla ākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	auvai, auvai eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.	(22)
56.	akarattu impar yakarap puḷḷiyum
	aieṉ neṭuñciṉai meypeṟat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) akarattu impar yakarap puḷḷiyum - akarattiṉ piṉṉar ikaramēyaṉṟi yakaramākiya puḷḷiyum, ai eṉ neṭuñciṉai mey peṟattōṉṟum - ai eṉappaṭṭa neṭṭeḻuttām vaṭivupeṟat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aivaṉam, ayvaṉam eṉavarum.
'meypeṟat tōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, akarattiṉ piṉṉar ukaramēyaṉṟi, vakarappuḷḷiyum aukārampōla varumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	auvai, avvai eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.	(23)
aikāram moḻikkaṇ orumāttirai icaittal
57.	ōraḷa pākum iṭaṉumā ruṇṭē
	tēruṅ kālai moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, eṉ nutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, uyirkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṟku māttiraic curukkam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tēruṅkālai moḻivayiṉ ōr aḷapu ākum iṭaṉum uṇṭu - aikāram ārāyuṅkālattu moḻikkaṇ ōr aḷapāy niṟkum iṭamum uṇṭu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	iṭaiyaṉ, maṭaiyaṉ eṉavarum.
'tēruṅ kālai' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutaṟkaṇ curuṅkāteṉpatu koḷka. ñīiṭaṉñī eṉṟataṉāṉ ik kuṟukkam ciṟupāṉmaiyeṉpatu koḷka.	(24)
yakarattiṟku ikaram pōliyeḻuttāka varutal
58.	ikara yakaram iṟuti viravum.
ituvum oru pōliyeḻuttu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṟuti ikara yakaram viravum - ikaravīṟṟu moḻikkaṇ yakaramum (atupōla) ikaramum viravivarum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāy, nāi 
eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.	(25)
uyirpaṉṉiraṇṭum moḻimuta lātal
59.	paṉṉīr uyirum moḻimuta lākum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttu moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉṉīr uyirum moḻimutal ākum - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aṭai, āṭai, ilai, īyam, ural, ūrti, eḻu, ēṇi, aivaṉam, oḷi, ōkkam, auviyam eṉa varum.	(26)
uyiroṭu kūṭiyallatu mey mutalākāmai
60.	uyirmey allaṉa moḻimutal ākā.
itu, meyyeḻuttu moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey allaṉa moḻi mutal ākā - uyiroṭu kūṭiya meyyallātaṉavākiya taṉimeykaḷ moḻikku mutalākā; uyiroṭu kūṭiya meykaḷ moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
īṇṭu uyirmey yeṉpatu vēṟṟumainayam karutiyeṉa vuṇarka. īṇṭu oṟṟumai karutil,
"katana pamaeṉum āvain teḻuttum
ellā vuyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē" 	 (moḻimarapu 28)
eṉac cūttiram curuṅka 1varuvataṉṟi, itaṉāṟ collappaṭṭa aṟupatu uyirmeyyiṉai 2eṭuttōta vēṇṭic cūttiram parakka varumeṉpatu.			(27)
1 (pāṭam) 'ōtumataṉṟi' eṉpatu.
2 (pāṭam) 'eṭuttōta vēṇṭil' eṉpatu.
moḻikku mutalām eṉappaṭṭa uyirmeykaṭku varaiyaṟai
61.	katana pamaeṉum āvain teḻuttum
	ellā uyiroṭuñ cellumār mutalē.
itu, mēl mutalām eṉṉappaṭṭa uyirmeykaṭku varaiyaṟai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ta na pa ma eṉum a aintu eḻuttum - ka ta na pa ma eṉṟu collappaṭṭa aintu taṉi meyyeḻuttum, ellā uyiroṭum mutal cellum - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirōṭum moḻikku mutalātaṟkuc cellum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalai, kālai, kiḷi, kīri, kuṭi, kūṭu, keṇṭai, kēḻal, kaitai, koṇṭal, kōṭai, keḷavai eṉavum; tantai, tāṭi, tiṟṟi, tīmai, tuṇi, tūṇi, teṟṟi, tēvar, taiyal, toṇṭai, tōṭu, teḷavai eṉavum; naṭam, nārai, nilam, nīr, nuḻai, nūl, neytal, nēyam, naikai, noyyaṉa, nōkkam, nauvi eṉavum; paṭai, pāṭi, piṭi, pīṭam, pukaḻ, pūmi, peṭai, pēṭi, paital, poṉ, pōtakam, pauvam eṉavum; maṭam, mālai, miṭaṟu, mīṉam, mukam, mūtūr, melintatu, mēṉi, maiyal, moḻi, mōtakam, mauval eṉavum varum.
'mutaṟku' eṉpataṉ nāṉkām urupu vikārattāṉ tokkatu.	(28)
62.	cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟō raṟṟē
	aai aueṉum mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) cakarak kiḷaviyum avaṟṟuḷ ōr aṟṟu - cakaramākiya eḻuttum mēṟcollappaṭṭavaipōla ellā uyiroṭum moḻikku mutalām, a ai au eṉum mūṉṟu alaṅkaṭai - a ai au eṉṉum mūṉṟum allāviṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cālai, cilai, cīṟuka, curumpu, cūḻka, ceykai, cēvaṭi, corika, cōṟu eṉa varum.
cakaṭam eṉavum, caiyam eṉavum vilakkiṉavum varumāleṉiṉ, avaṟṟuḷ āriyaccitai vallātaṉa 'kaṭicol illai' (col. 452) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷka.	(29)
63.	uū oō eṉṉum nāṉkuyir
	vaeṉ eḻuttoṭu varuta lillai.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō eṉṉum nāṉku uyir - u ū o ō eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟa nāṉku uyirum, va eṉ eḻuttoṭu varutal illai - va eṉṉum meyyeḻuttoṭu moḻi mutalil varutalillai; piṟa uyirkaḷ varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	vaḷai, vāḷi, viḷari, vīṭu, veḷḷi, vēr, vaiyam, vautaṭīvu eṉa varum.	(30)
64.	ā e
	oeṉum mūvuyir ñakārat turiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ā e o eṉum mū uyir ñakārattu uriya - ā e o eṉṟu collappaṭum mūvuyirkaḷ ñakāra oṟṟoṭu mutalātaṟku uriya; piṟa uyir uriyavalla e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ñālam, ñekiḻi, ñoḷkiṟṟu eṉavarum.
ñaḻiyiṟṟu eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāleṉiṉ, avai *iḻivaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka.	(31)
*(pāṭam) 'aḻivaḻakkeṉṟu maṟukka' eṉpatu.
65.	āvō ṭallatu yakara mutalātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) āvōṭu allatu yakaram mutalātu - ākārattōṭu allatu yakaram mutalākātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	yāṉ eṉa varum.
yavaṉar eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa vilakkiṉavum varumāleṉiṉ, avai āriyac citaiveṉṟu maṟukka.	(32)
moḻimutal ākātaṉavum tampeyar kūṟutaṟkaṇ mutalātal
66.	mutalā ēṉa tampeyar mutalum.
itu, moḻikku mutalākātaṉavum orōvaḻi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mutalā ēṉa - moḻikku mutalākāta oḻinta meykaḷum, tam peyar mutalum - (av veḻuttukkaḷ) tam peyar kūṟutaṟku mutalām e-ṟu.
mutalāyiṉa mey katanapamakkaḷum, vakaramum, cakaramum, ñakaramum, yakaramum eṉa ivai. mutalākāta mey ṅakaramum, ṭakaramum, ṇakaramum, rakaramum, lakaramum, ḻakaramum, ḷakaramum, ṟakaramum, ṉakaramum eṉa ivai.
e-ṭu:	avai tam peyarkku mutalāmāṟu: ṅakkaḷaintār, ṭapperitu, ṇannaṉṟu eṉṟāṟpōla oṭṭik koḷka.
iṉi 'ēṉa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, mutalām eṉṉappaṭṭa oṉpatu uyir meyyum paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum tam peyar kūṟum vaḻiyum moḻikku mutalām eṉakkoḷka.
e-ṭu: kakkaḷaintār, tapperitu, akkuṟitu, āvalitu eṉṟāṟ pōla oṭṭikkoḷka.	(33)
moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaram
67.	kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ
	oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum.
itu, kuṟṟiyalukaram moḻikku mutalāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟaippeyar maruṅkiṉ - kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟaip peyariṭattu, oṟṟiya nakaramicai nakaramoṭu mutalum - oṟṟāy niṉṟa nakarattiṉ mēlāya nakarattoṭu moḻikku mutalām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nuntai eṉa varum.
ivvāṟu mutalākkam kūṟavē, moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku iṭamum paṟṟukkōṭum kūṟiyavāṟāyiṟṟu. iṭam nuntai eṉṉum muṟaippeyar. paṟṟukkōṭu nakaramicai nakaram.	(34)
atu muṟṟukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟupaṭāmai
68.	muṟṟiya lukaramoṭu poruḷvēṟu paṭāatu
	appeyar maruṅkiṉ nilaiyiya lāṉa.
itu, mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai.
(i-ḷ.) muṟṟiyalukaramoṭu poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu - (am mutaṟkaṭ kuṟṟiyalukaram āṇṭu itaḻkuvittuk kūṟum vaḻi) muṟṟukarat toṭu piṟa ukarampōlap poruḷ vēṟupaṭātu; (yāṇṭeṉiṉ,) ap peyar maruṅkiṉ nilai iyalāṉa - am muṟaippeyariṭattut tāṉ niṟṟaṟkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāku eṉavum, naku eṉavum kuṟukiyum, kuṟukātum niṉṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla, nuntai eṉṟu kuṟukkamāya vaḻiyum, itaḻ kuvittuk kūṟak kuṟukātavaḻiyum, poruḷ vēṟupaṭāta vāṟu aṟika.
iṉi 'iraṭṭuṟamoḻital' eṉpataṉāṉ, poruḷ eṉṟataṉai iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum ākki, piṟa ukaraṅkaḷpōla īṇṭai ukaram iṭaṉum paṟṟukkōṭum vēṟupaṭātu eṉpatu koḷka.	(35)
aukāram nīṅkalāṉa uyirkaḷ moḻikku īṟātal
69.	uyirau eñciya iṟuti yākum.
iḵtu, uyir moḻikku īṟāmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir au eñciya iṟuti ākum - uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ aukāram oḻintavai moḻikku īṟām, aukārantāṉ īṟākātu e-ṟu.
iḵtu uyirkkum uyirmeykkum potu.
e-ṭu:	āa, ā, īi, ī, ūu, ū, ēe, ē, aii, ai, ōo, ō eṉa uyir īṟāyiṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟeḻuttaintum aḷapeṭai vakaiyāṉ īṟāyiṉa.
uyirmeykaḷum, mēlvaraiyaṟai kūṟātaṉavākiya akara ākāra ikara īkāra aikāraṅkaḷōṭu iyaintaṉa īṇṭē koḷka.
e-ṭu:	viḷa, palā,kiḷi, kurī, paṉai eṉa varum.	(36)
aukāram kavavōṭu iyaintu īṟātal
70.	kavavō ṭiyaiyiṉ auvu mākum.
iḵtu, īṟākāteṉṟa aukāram iṉṉuḻi ām eṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) kava ōṭu iyaiyiṉ - kakara vakaramākiya meykaḷoṭu poruntiṉ, auvum ākum - muṉ īṟākā teṉṟa aukāramum īṟām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: kau, vau eṉa varum.	(37)
ekaram meyyōṭiyaintu īṟākāmai
71.	eeṉa varumuyir meyyī ṟākātu.
iḵtu, ekaram tāṉē niṉṟavaḻiyaṉṟi meyyoṭukūṭi īṟākā teṉa varaiyaṟai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e eṉa varum uyir - e eṉṟu colla varum uyir, mey īṟu ākātu - tāṉē īṟāvataṉṟi meyyōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu eṅiṟu.	(38)
okaram nakaramey allavaṟṟōṭu iyaintu īṟākāmai
72.	ovvum aṟṟē navvalaṅ kaṭaiyē.
ituvum varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) ovvum aṟṟu - okaramum tāṉē niṉṟu īṟāva taṉṟi meyyōṭu iyaintu īṟākātu, na alaṅkaṭai - nakara meyyō ṭallāta viṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nau eṉa varum.	(39)
ēkāra ōkāraṅkaḷ ñakarattōṭu iyaintu īṟākāmai
73.	ēō eṉumuyir ñakārat tillai.
ituvum varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) ē ō eṉum uyir ñakārattu illai - ē ō eṉṟu collappaṭum uyir (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum īṟāvataṉṟi) ñakarattōṭu īṟāvatillai e-ṟu.
tāmē yātal muṉṉē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāvaṉavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa voḻiya iṟavātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(40)
ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ nakaravakaraṅkaḷōṭu iyaintu īṟākāmai
74.	uū kāram navavoṭu navilā.
ituvum varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) u ūkāram na va oṭu navilā - ukara ūkāraṅkaḷ (tāmē niṉṟum piṟa meykaḷoṭu niṉṟum payilvataṉṟi) nakara vakaraṅkaḷoṭu payilā e-ṟu.
tāmē īṟātal mēlē kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. piṟa meykaḷōṭu īṟāmavaṟṟuḷ vaḻakkiṟantaṉa vallātaṉa vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
'navilā' eṉṟataṉāṟ ciṟupāṉmai novvu kavvu eṉṟu (ukaram) vakārattōṭu īṟātal koḷka. iṉṉum itaṉāṉē ciṟupāṉmai nakarattōṭu varavu uṇṭēṉum koḷka.	(41)
uccakāram irumoḻikkē urittātal
75.	ucca kāram irumoḻik kurittē.
ituvum atu orōvaḻi varaiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāram irumoḻikku urittu - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakaram *irumoḻikku īṟākum; palamoḻikku īṟākātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ucu, mucu eṉa varum. pacuveṉpatu āriyac citaivu.	(42)
*(pāṭam) 'irumoḻikku allātu' eṉpatu.
uppakāram orumoḻikkē urittātalum, ataṉporuḷ vēṟupāṭum
76.	uppa kāram oṉṟeṉa moḻipa
	iruvayi ṉilaiyum poruṭṭā kummē.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟaiyum moḻiyatu poruḷ vēṟupāṭum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāram oṉṟu eṉa moḻipa - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakaram oru moḻikku īṟām eṉṟu colluvar, iruvayiṉ nilaiyum poruṭṭu ākum - atutāṉ taṉviṉai piṟaviṉai eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭat tum nilaipeṟum poruṇmaittām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tapu eṉa varum. itu paṭuttuc colla, nī cā eṉat taṉ viṉaiyām. eṭuttuc colla, nī oṉṟaṉaic cāvippi eṉap piṟa viṉaiyām.	(43)
moḻikku īṟākā uyirmeyyum tampeyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātal
77.	eñciya vellām eñcutal ilavē.
itu, moḻikku īṟākā uyirmeyyum orōvaḻi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eñciya ellām eñcutal ila - moḻikku īṟākātu niṉṟa uyirmeykaḷellām tam peyar kūṟumvaḻi īṟātaṟku oḻipu ila e-ṟu.
eñciya uyirmeyyāvaṉa: aukāram kakāra vakāraṅkaḷai oḻinta meyyōṭu iyainta uyirmeyyum, ekaram ellā meyyōṭum iyainta uyirmeyyum, okaram nakaram oḻinta meyyōṭu iyainta uyirmeyyum, ēkāra ōkāram ñakārattōṭu iyainta uyir meyyum, u ūkāram nakara vakaraṅkaḷōṭu iyainta uyirmeyyum eṉa ivai.
e-ṭu:	tam peyarkku īṟu āmāṟu: ṅauk kaḷaintār eṉavum, kek kaḷaintār eṉavum, kok kaḷaintār eṉavum, ñēk kaḷaintār eṉavum, ñōk kaḷaintār eṉavum, nuk kaḷaintār eṉavum, nūk kaḷaintār eṉavum, vuk kaḷaintār eṉavum, vūk kaḷaintār eṉavum varum.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, moḻikku īṟāy niṉṟa uyir meykaḷum tam peyar kūṟum vaḻiyum īṟām eṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kak kaḷaintār, kā valitu eṉavum, ak kaḷaintār, ā valitu eṉavum ivvāṟē oṭṭik koḷka.
taṉṉai uṇara niṉṟavaḻi, moḻikaṭku itu karuviyāka īṟṟakattu muṭipu 'oṉṟiṉa muṭittalāṟ' koḷka.	(44)
moḻikku īṟām taṉimeykaḷ
78.	ñaṇanama ṉayarala vaḻaḷa veṉṉum
	appati ṉoṉṟē puḷḷi yiṟuti.
itu, taṉimeykaḷuḷ moḻikku īṟāvaṉa kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṉum ap patiṉoṉṟē - ña ṇa na ma ṉa ya ra la va ḻa ḷa eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭa ap patiṉoṉṟumē, puḷḷi iṟuti - puḷḷikaḷil moḻikku īṟāvaṉa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ, maṇ, porun, tirum, poṉ, vēy, vēr, vēl, tev, vīḻ, vēḷ eṉa varum.
ṉakārattai īṟṟu vaiyātu, makarattoṭu vaittatu ataṉ mayakka iyaipu nōkki eṉṟuṇarka.	(45)
nakaramey irumoḻikkē īṟātal
79.	ucca kāramoṭu nakāram civaṇum.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) uccakāramoṭu nakāram civaṇum - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya cakārattōṭē nakāram porunti aḵtu irumoḻikku īṟāyavāṟu pōlat tāṉum irumoḻikku īṟām e-ṟu. 
e-ṭu:	porun, verin eṉa varum.	(46)
ñakaramey orumoḻikkē īṟātal
80.	uppa kāramoṭu ñakāraiyum aṟṟē
	apporuḷ iraṭṭā tivaṇai yāṉa.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) uppakāramoṭu ñakāraiyum aṟṟu - ukarattoṭu kūṭiya pakarattoṭu ñakāramum attaṉmaittāy orumoḻikku īṟām, ivaṇaiyāṉa a poruḷ iraṭṭātu - ivviṭattu ataṉ poruḷ av vuppakāram pōla iruporuḷ paṭātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ eṉa varum.
ñakāram orumoḻikku īṟātaliṉ, nakarattiṉpiṉ kūṟappaṭṭatu.(47)
vakaramey īṟām coṟkaḷ nāṉkē ātal
81.	vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu.
ituvum moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) vakarak kiḷavi nāṉmoḻi īṟṟatu - vakaramākiya eḻuttu nāṉkumoḻi īṟṟatām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	av, iv, uv, tev eṉa varum.	(48)
makara īṟṟoṭu mayaṅkāta ṉakara īṟṟuttoṭar moḻikaḷ
oṉpatu eṉal
82.	makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta
	ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵ teṉpa
	pukaraṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa.
ituvum orōvaḻi moḻivaraiyaṟai.
(i-ḷ.) pukar aṟak kiḷanta aḵṟiṇai mēṉa - kuṟṟam aṟac collappaṭṭa aḵṟiṇaiyiṭattu, makarat toṭarmoḻi mayaṅkutal varainta - makaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻiyoṭu mayaṅkāteṉṟu varaiyaṟukkappaṭṭa, ṉakarat toṭarmoḻi oṉpaḵtu eṉpa - ṉakaravīṟṟut toṭarmoḻi oṉpateṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nilaṉ nilam, pilaṉ pilam eṉṟāṟpōlvaṉa mayaṅkuvaṉa. iṉi mayaṅkātaṉa: ekiṉ, cekiṉ, viḻaṉ, payiṉ, aḻaṉ, puḻaṉ, kuyiṉ, kaṭāṉ, vayāṉ eṉa varum.
ivaṟṟuḷ tiripuṭaiyaṉa kaḷaika. oṉpaḵtu eṉṉum āytam ceyyuḷ vikāram. aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟatu īṇṭu aḵṟiṇaip peyariṉai.	(49)
moḻimarapu muṟṟiṟṟu.

3
piṟappiyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ  piṟappiyal  eṉṉum peyarttu. itaṉai nūṉmarapiṉ piṉṉē vaikkaveṉiṉ, cārpil tōṟṟat teḻuttum taṉimeyyum moḻimarapiṭai uṇarttip piṟappu uṇartta vēṇṭutaliṉ, moḻi marapiṉ piṉṉatāyiṟṟu.
eḻuttukkaḷatu potuppiṟappu : iṭamum muyaṟciyum
83.	unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟit
	talaiyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilaiip
	pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum
	aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ
	uṟuppuṟ ṟamaiya neṟippaṭa nāṭi
	ellā veḻuttuñ colluṅ kālaip
	piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟuvē ṟiyala
	tiṟappaṭat teriyuṅ kāṭci yāṉa.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, eḻuttukkaḷatu potup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum neṟippaṭa nāṭi collum kālai - tamiḻeḻuttukkaḷellām oruvaṉ muṟaippaṭa ārāyntu tammaic colluṅkālattu, unti mutalā muntuvaḷi tōṉṟi - koppūḻ aṭiyāka mēlē kiḷarkiṉṟa utāṉaṉ eṉṉum peyaraiyuṭaiya vāyut tōṉṟi, talaiyiṉum miṭaṟṟiṉum neñciṉum nilaii - talaiyiṉ kaṇṇum miṭaṟṟiṉkaṇṇum neñciṉkaṇṇum nilai peṟṟu, pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṇmuṟai nilaiyāṉ uṟuppu uṟṟu amaiya - (talaiyum miṭaṟum neñcum eṉṉum mūṉṟa ṉōṭum) pallum itaḻum nāvum mūkkum aṇṇamum uḷappaṭa eṭṭākiya muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya iṭaṅkaḷiṉ ōr uṟuppōṭu ōr uṟupput tammiṟ porunti amaitipeṟa, piṟappiṉ ākkam vēṟu vēṟu iyala - piṟappiṉatu ākkam vēṟu vēṟu pulappaṭṭu vaḻaṅkutaluṭaiya, tiṟappaṭat teriyum kāṭciyāṉa - kūṟupāṭuḷatāka ārāyum aṟiviṭattu e-ṟu.
itaḻpōṟalāṉ vāy itaḻeṉappaṭṭatu. 'ellā eḻuttum' eṉṉum eḻuvāykkup 'piṟappi ṉākkam vēṟuvēṟu iyala' eṉpataṉai orucoṉ ṉīrmaippaṭuttip payaṉilaiyākkuka.	(1)
uyireḻuttu miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyāṉ icaittal
84.	avvaḻip
	paṉṉī ruyirum tannilai tiriyā
	miṭaṟṟup piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icaikkum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttiṟkup potuppiṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a vaḻi - avviṭattu, paṉṉīr uyirum tam nilai tiriyā - paṉṉiraṇṭu uyireḻuttum tattam nilaiyiṉ tiriyāvāy, miṭaṟṟu piṟanta vaḷiyiṉ icaikkum - miṭaṟṟiṉkaṭ piṟanta vaḷiyāṉ olikkum e-ṟu.
'tannilai tiriyā' eṉṟataṉāṉ, kuṟṟiyalikaram, kuṟṟiyalukaram tannilai tiriyumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
uyireḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappup piṟavi uṇarttutal
85.	avaṟṟuḷ
	aā āyiraṇ ṭaṅkān tiyalum.
iḵtu, uyireḻuttukkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkuc ciṟappup piṟavi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyir paṉṉiraṇṭa ṉuḷ, a ā āyiraṇṭu aṅkāntu iyalum - akara ākāraṅkaḷākiya av viraṇṭum aṅkāntu colla aḵtu iṭamākap piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(3)
86.	iī eē aiyeṉa icaikkum
	appāl aintum avaṟṟō raṉṉa
	avaitām
	aṇpal mutalnā viḷimpuṟal uṭaiya.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) i ī e ē ai eṉa icaikkum a pāl aintum - i ī e ē ai eṉṟu collappaṭum ak kūṟṟu aintum, avaṟṟu ōr aṉṉa - mēṟkūṟiya akara ākāraṅkaḷ pōla aṅkāntu collap piṟakkum, avaitām palaṇ nāmutal viḷimpu uṟal uṭaiya - avaitām (avvāṟu collap piṟakkumiṭattup) palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉai nāviṉatu aṭiyiṉ viḷimpu ceṉṟu uṟutalai yuṭaiya e-ṟu.	(4)
87.	uū oō aueṉa icaikkum
	appāl aintum itaḻkuvin tiyalum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) u ū o ō au eṉa icaikkum a pāl aintum - u ū o ō au eṉac colla icaikkum ak kūṟṟu aintum, itaḻ kuvintu iyalum - itaḻ kuvittuc colla naṭakkum e-ṟu.	(5)
uyir, mey ivaṟṟiṉ piṟavikkup puṟaṉaṭai
88.	tattan tiripē ciṟiya veṉpa.
itu, muṉkūṟiya uyirkkum mēṟkūṟum meykkum oru puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) tamtam tiripu ciṟiya eṉpa - (eḻuttukkaḷ oru tāṉattuk kūṭip piṟakku meṉappaṭṭaṉa; avvāṟu kūṭip piṟappiṉum,) tattam vēṟupāṭukaḷaic ciṟiya vēṟupāṭukaḷeṉṟu colluvar. av vēṟupāṭu aṟintukoḷka e-ṟu.	(6)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ka, ṅa - meykaḷ
89.	kakāra ṅakāram mutalnā aṇṇam.
itu, meykaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟavi āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kakāram ṅakāram nāmutal aṇṇam (mutal) - kakāramum ṅakāramum nāmutalum aṇṇamutalum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu. 	(7)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ca, ña - meykaḷ
90.	cakāra ñakāram iṭainā aṇṇam.
(i-ḷ.) cakāram ñakāram nāiṭai aṇṇam (iṭai) - cakāramum ñakāramum nāvatu iṭaiyum aṇṇattatu iṭaiyum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(8)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ṭa, ṇa - meykaḷ
91.	ṭakāra ṇakāram nuṉinā aṇṇam.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṭakāram ṇakāram nānuṉi aṇṇam (nuṉi) - ṭakāramum ṇakāramum nāvatu nuṉiyum aṇṇattatu nuṉiyum uṟap piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(9)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - mēlaṉa āṟum mūvakaip piṟappiṉa ātal
92.	avvā ṟeḻuttum mūvakaip piṟappiṉa.
itu mēlaṉavaṟṟiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) a āṟu eḻuttum mūvakai piṟappiṉa - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa āṟu eḻuttum niraṉiṟai vakaiyāṉ (aṟuvakaip piṟappiṉa alla) mūvakaip piṟappiṉa e-ṟu.	(10)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ta, na - meykaḷ
93.	aṇṇam naṇṇiya palmutal maruṅkiṉ
	nānuṉi parantu meyyuṟa voṟṟat
	tāmiṉitu piṟakkun takāra nakāram.
ituvum meykaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam naṇṇiya pal mutal maruṅkiṉ - aṇṇattaip poruntiya palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattiṉkaṇṇē, nānuṉi parantu mey uṟa oṟṟa - nāviṉatu nuṉi parantu vaṭivai uṟumpaṭi oṟṟa, tām iṉitu piṟakkum - tām iṉitākap piṟakkum, takāram nakāram - takāramum nakāramum e-ṟu.
muṉṉē 'uṟuppuṟ ṟamaiya' (83) eṉṟu vaittup piṉṉum 'meyyuṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ellā eḻuttukkaḷum meyyuṟṟapōtē iṉitu piṟappaveṉpatu koḷka.	(11)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ṟa, ṉa - meykaḷ
94.	aṇari nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟa
	ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ āyiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇari nuṉinā aṇṇam oṟṟa - aṇarntu nuṉinā aṇṇattaic ceṉṟu oṟṟa, ṟaḵkāṉ ṉaḵkāṉ a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - ṟakāra ṉakāramākiya av viraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.
iṅkuniṉṟum neṭuṅkaṇakku muṟaimai viṭṭu nā atikāram paṭṭatu kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟateṉa vuṇarka.	(12)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - ra, ḻa - meykaḷ
95.	nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa
	rakāra ḻakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nuṉinā aṇari aṇṇam varuṭa - nuṉinā aṇarntu aṇṇattait taṭava, rakāra ḻakāram a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - rakāra ḻakāramākiya avviraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(13)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - la, ḷa - meykaḷ
96.	nāviḷimpu vīṅki yaṇpal mutaluṟa
	āvayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟavum varuṭavum
	lakāra ḷakāramā yiraṇṭum piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) nā viḷimpu vīṅki pal aṇ mutal uṟa - nāviṉatu viḷimpu taṭittup palliṉatu aṇiya iṭattaip porunta, a vayiṉ aṇṇam oṟṟa lakāramum varuṭa ḷakāramum a iraṇṭum piṟakkum - avviṭattu (mutalnā) av vaṇṇattai oṟṟa lakāramum ataṉait taṭava ḷakāramumāka av viraṇṭum piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(14)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - pa, ma - meykaḷ
97.	itaḻiyaintu piṟakkum pakāra makāram.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) itaḻ iyaintu piṟakkum pakāram makāram - kīḻ itaḻum mēl itaḻum tammil iyaiyap piṟakkum pakāramum makāramum e-ṟu.	(15)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - vakāramey
98.	pallitaḻ iyaiya vakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) pal itaḻ iyaiya vakāram piṟakkum - mēṟpallum kīḻ itaḻum tammil iyaiya vakāram piṟakkum e-ṟu.	(16)
meyyeḻuttukkaṭkuc ciṟappuppiṟavi āmāṟu - yakāramey
99.	aṇṇañ cērnta miṭaṟṟeḻu vaḷiyicai
	kaṇṇuṟ ṟaṭaiya yakāram piṟakkum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aṇṇam cērnta miṭaṟṟu eḻu vaḷiicai kaṇ uṟṟu aṭaiya yakāram piṟakkum - aṇṇattai nāc cērnta viṭattu miṭaṟṟiṉiṉṟum eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya icai av vaṇṇattai aṇaintu ceṟiya yakāram piṟakkum. 	(17)
melleḻuttukkaḷ mūkkuvaḷiyicaiyāṉ tōṉṟutal
100.	melleḻut tāṟum piṟappiṉ ākkañ
	colliya paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa vāyiṉum
	mūkkiṉ vaḷiyicai yāppuṟat tōṉṟum.
itu, melleḻuttiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu āṟum piṟappiṉ ākkam colliya paḷḷi nilaiyiṉa āyiṉum - melleḻuttukkaḷ āṟum tattam piṟappiṉatu ākkam mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattē nilai peṟṟaṉa vāyiṉum, mūkkiṉ vaḷi icai yāppuṟa tōṉṟum - avai mūkkiṉkaṇ ṇuḷatākiya vaḷiyatu icaiyāṉ yāppuṟat tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
'yāppuṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, iṭaiyeḻuttiṟku miṭaṟṟuvaḷiyum, valleḻuttiṟkut talaivaḷiyum koḷka.	(18)
cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttukkaḷ piṟakkumāṟu
101.	cārntuvari ṉallatu tamakkiyal pilaveṉat
	tērntuveḷip paṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum
	tattañ cārpiṟ piṟappoṭu civaṇi
	otta kāṭciyiṉ tammiyal piyalum.
itu, cārpiṉ tōṟṟattu eḻuttiṟkup piṟappu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cārntuvariṉ allatu tamakku iyalpu ila eṉat tērntu veḷippaṭutta ēṉai mūṉṟum - cilavaṟṟaic cārntu variṉ allatu tamakkut tāmē varum iyalpu ilaveṉṟu ārāyntu veḷippaṭuttap paṭṭa oḻinta mūṉṟum, tamtam cārpiṉ piṟappoṭu civaṇi otta kāṭci yiṉ tam iyalpu iyalum - tattamakkuc cārpākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟap piṭattē piṟattaloṭu porunti avviṭattē tamakkuriya iyalpiṉ naṭakkum e-ṟu.
'otta kāṭci' eṉṟataṉāṉ, āytattiṟkuk kuṟṟeḻuttuc cārpēyeṉiṉum talaivaḷiyāṟ piṟattaliṉ, uyiroṭu puṇarnta valleḻuttuc cārpākavē piṟakkumeṉpatu koḷka. 'tammiyal piyalum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḷapeṭaiyum uyirmeyyum tamakku aṭiyākiya eḻuttukkaḷatu piṟappiṭamē iṭamāka varumeṉpatu koḷka.	(19)
uḷniṉṟeḻum vaḷiyicai yārāycci maṟaiyiṉkaṇṇatu eṉal
102.	ellā veḻuttum veḷippaṭak kiḷantu
	colliya paḷḷi yeḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ
	piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi uṟaḻcci vārat(tu)
	akatteḻu vaḷiyicai yariltapa nāṭi
	aḷapiṟ kōṭal antaṇar maṟaittē.
iḵtu, ellā eḻuttiṟkum āvatoru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā eḻuttum - ellā eḻuttukkaḷum, veḷippaṭa kiḷantu colliya paḷḷi - veḷippaṭa vitantu collappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē, eḻutaru vaḷiyiṉ - eḻukiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉē, piṟappoṭu viṭuvaḻi - tām piṟakkun toḻiluṭaiyavātaloṭu tammaic collum iṭattu, uṟaḻcci vārattu akattu eḻuvaḷi icai - tiritaruṅ kūṟṟaiyuṭaiya uḷniṉṟu eḻum vaḷiyāṉāya icaiyai, aril tapa nāṭi - piṇakkamaṟa ārāyntu, aḷapiṉ kōṭal - māttirai varaiyaṟaiyāṟ kōṭal, antaṇar maṟaittu - pārppār vētattuk kaṇṇatu e-ṟu.
'uṟaḻcci vāram' eṉṟatu, untimutalā eḻum vaḷi talaikāṟum ceṉṟu mīṇṭum neñciṉkaṇ nilaipeṟutalai eṉak koḷka. 'vaḷi' eṉṉātu 'vaḷiyicai' eṉṟatu, avvāṟu neñciṉkaṇ nilaipeṟum aḷavē vaḷi eṉappaṭum; piṉṉai neñciṉiṉṟum eḻuvuḻiyellām vaḷittaṉmai *tirintu eḻuttām taṉmaiyatām eṉpatu viḷakki niṉṟatu.	(20)
*(pāṭam) 'cārntu' eṉpatu.
mēlaic cūttirattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭai 
(puṟatticaikkum oli poruḷpaṭuvataṟkē māttirai kūṟappaṭum eṉṟal)
103.	aḵtivaṇ nuvalā teḻuntupuṟat ticaikkum
	meyteri vaḷiyicai aḷapunuvaṉ ṟiciṉē.
itu, mēṟcūttirattiṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵtu ivaṇ nuvalātu - ('akatteḻu vaḷiyicai aḷapiṟ kōṭalākiya') ataṉai in nūliṭattuc collātu, eḻuntu puṟattu icaikkum - (akattiṉiṉṟu) eḻuntu puṟattup pōntu icaikkum, mey teri vaḷi icai aḷapu - poruṇmai terikiṉṟa vaḷiyāṉāya icaiyatu māttiraiyiṉai, nuvaṉṟiciṉ - yāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟiṉēṉ e-ṟu.
maṟṟu, iḵtu "aḷapiṟ kōṭa lantaṇar maṟaittu" (piṟappiyal 20) eṉavē peṟappaṭṭataṉṟōveṉiṉ, 'antaṇar maṟaittu' eṉpatu piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟutaṟkum, piṟaṉkōṭ kūṟṟu nērntu uṭampaṭutaṟkum oppak kiṭantamaiyiṉ av vaiyam tīrtaṟkuk kūṟiṉāreṉpatu. puṟattu icaippataṉ muṉṉar, akattu icaikkum vaḷi yicaiyai am maṟaikkaṇ ōr eḻuttiṟku mūṉṟu nilai uḷatākak kūṟum (ataṉ āciriyaṉ); aḵtu āmāṟu aṟintu koḷka.
'meyteri' eṉṟataṉāṉ, muṟku mutaliyaṉa muyaṟciyāṟ piṟakkumeṉiṉum, poruḷ teriyā nilaimaiya vākaliṉ avaṟṟiṟku aḷapu kūṟārāyiṉāreṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
'nuvaṉṟu' eṉpatu īṇṭu iṟantakālat taṉmai viṉai.	(21)

piṟappiyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

4
puṇariyal
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, moḻikaḷ, (mēṟ ceykai yōttukkaḷuḷ) puṇartaṟkuriya karuviyiṉ iyalpu kūṟiṉamai yiṉ puṇariyal eṉappaṭṭatu. mēl moḻimarapiṟ kūṟiya moḻikaḷ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttiṉamaiyiṉ moḻimarapiṉōṭu iyaipuṭait tāyiṟṟu.
ellāc coṟkaḷiṉ īṟum mutalum meyyum uyirumāy aṭaṅkutal
104.	mūṉṟutalai yiṭṭa muppatiṟ ṟeḻuttiṉ
	iraṇṭutalai yiṭṭa mutalā kirupaḵtu
	aṟunāṉ kīṟoṭu neṟiniṉ ṟiyalum
	ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum
	meyyē yuyireṉṟu āyī riyala.
it talaic cūttiram eṉnutaliṟṟō veṉiṉ, moḻimarapiṉuḷ mutalum īṟum kūṟiyavaḻi uyirum meyyum uyir meyyum eṉa mūṉṟāy virintu niṉṟataṉai uyirum meyyum eṉa iraṇṭākat tokuttalāṉum, avvaḻi irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu moḻikku mutal, irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu moḻikku īṟu eṉak kūṟaliṉ, muppattu mūṉṟu eṉappaṭṭa eḻuttu nāṟpattāṟu āvaṉapōl virintataṉai avaiyellām muppattu mūṉṟaṉuḷḷaṉavē eṉat tokuttalāṉum virintatu tokuttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mūṉṟu talaiyiṭṭa muppatiṟṟu eḻuttiṉ - mūṉṟaṉai muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa muppatākiya eḻuttiṉuḷ, iraṇṭu talaiyiṭṭa mutal āku irupaḵtu - av viraṇṭaṉai muṭivilē yiṭappaṭṭa moḻikku mutalāya irupatum, aṟunāṉku īṟoṭu neṟi niṉṟu iyalum - irupattu nāṉku īṟṟōṭu vaḻakku neṟikkaṇ niṉṟu naṭakkum, ellā moḻikkum iṟutiyum mutalum - mūvakai moḻikkum īṟum mutalu māvaṉa, mey uyir eṉṟu a īr iyala - meyyum uyirumākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉai yuṭaiya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maram, ilai, āl, viḷa eṉa meyyum uyirum mutalum īṟum āyiṉa.
irupattiraṇṭu eḻuttu mutalāvaṉa  paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum oṉpatu uyirmeyyum moḻimutaṟ kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivai. irupattu nāṉku eḻuttu īṟāvaṉa paṉṉiraṇṭu uyirum patiṉoru puḷḷiyum īṟṟuk kuṟṟiyalukaramum eṉa ivai.
'īṟṟoṭu' eṉpatu vikārattāṉ tokkatu. mey mutaṟ kūṟiyavata ṉāṉ, nālvakaip puṇarcciyum meykkaṇ nikaḻumāṟu uyirkkaṇ nikaḻāveṉpatu koḷka.	(1)
moḻiyīṟākiya mey puḷḷi peṟṟu niṟṟal
105.	avaṟṟuḷ
	meyyī ṟellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal.
itu, mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ taṉimeyyum mutalāvāṉ ceṉṟataṉai vilakkaliṉ, eytiyatu vilakkal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēl meyyum uyirum eṉṟu kūṟiya iraṇṭaṉuḷ, īṟu meyyellām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal - moḻikku īṟāya meyyellām puḷḷi peṟutaloṭu niṟkum; mutalāyavai yellām puḷḷiyiḻantu niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maram eṉa varum.
'moḻimutaṉ mey puḷḷiyoṭu nillātu' eṉṉātu, 'īṟel lām puḷḷiyoṭu nilaiyal' eṉa īṟṟiṉmēl vaittuk kūṟiyavata ṉāṉ, av vīṟṟiṉ mey uyir mutaṉmoḻi vanta iṭattu aḵtu ēṟa iṭaṅkoṭukkumeṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.	(2)
kuṟṟiyalukaraīṟum puḷḷiyīṟu pōla uyirēṟa iṭamkoṭuttal
106.	kuṟṟiya lukaramum aṟṟeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiyalukarattiṟku oru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukaramum aṟṟu eṉa moḻipa - īṟṟiṟ kuṟṟiya lukaramum (puḷḷi yīṟupōla uyirēṟa iṭam koṭukkum) at taṉmaittu eṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
immāṭṭēṟu orupuṭaic cēṟal eṉa vuṇarka.	(3)
uyirmeyyīṟu uyirīṟākak koḷḷappaṭutal
107.	uyirmey yīṟum uyirīṟ ṟiyaṟṟē.
itu, mēl "meyyē yuyireṉ ṟāyī riyala" (puṇariyal 1) eṉṟataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirmey īṟum uyir īṟṟu iyaṟṟu - uyirmey moḻiyīṟṟil niṉṟatuvum uyirīṟṟiṉ iyalpai yuṭaittu, iṭaiyil niṉṟatuvum uyiriṉ iyalpai yuṭaittu e-ṟu.
īṟum iṭaiyum uyiruḷ aṭaṅkumeṉavē, mutal meyyuḷ aṭaṅkum eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
itaṉāṉ, viḷa mutaliya uyirmey īṟellām akaravīṟu mutaliya uyirīṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkip puṇarccipeṟuvaṉa vāyiṉa. varaku eṉpuḻi iṭai niṉṟa rakara uyirmey akaramāy uyirttoṭarmoḻi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
īṇṭu uyirmey oṟṟumai nayattāṉ uyirmeyyeṉa vēṟu ōr eḻuttāvataṉṟi, īṟum iṭaiyum uyireṉa ōreḻuttāyum, mutal meyyeṉa ōreḻuttāyum niṉṟatāyiṟṟu.
ittuṇaiyum orumoḻi yilakkaṇaṅ kūṟaliṉ moḻimarapiṉ oḻipāyiṟṟu.	(4)
niṟutta collum kuṟittu varukiḷaviyum
108.	uyiriṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
	uyiriṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum
	meyyiṟu colmuṉ meyvaru vaḻiyumeṉ(ṟu)
	ivveṉa aṟiyak kiḷakkuṅ kālai
	niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyeṉ(ṟu)
	āyī riyala puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci mummoḻip puṇarcciyākātu, irumoḻip puṇarcciyā meṉpatūum, avai eḻuttuvakaiyāṉ nāṉkāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutalmoḻi varum iṭamum, uyir iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - uyirīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ uyirvaru vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyirmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, mey iṟu colmuṉ mey varu vaḻiyum - meyyīṟṟuc colmuṉ meymmutal moḻi varum iṭamum, iv eṉa aṟiya - (eṉṟu ap puṇarccivakai) ivaiyeṉa aṟiya, kiḷakkum kālai - āciriyar colluṅkālattu, niṟutta col kuṟittu varu kiḷavi eṉṟu a īr iyala - avai niṟutta collum ataṉ poruṇmaiyaik kuṟittuvarum collumākiya av viraṇṭu iyalpaiyuṭaiya, puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu - puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ā ī, ā valitu, āl ilai, āl vīḻntatu eṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.
viḷaviṉaik kuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu av vurupu kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyai nilaimoḻiyuḷ aṭakki irumoḻip puṇarcciyāy niṉṟavāṟu aṟika.	(5)
moḻipuṇar iyalpu mūṉṟu tiripum ōriyalpum ātal
109.	avaṟṟuḷ
	niṟutta colliṉ īṟā keḻuttoṭu
	kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi mutaleḻut tiyaiyap
	peyaroṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	peyaroṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	toḻiloṭu peyaraip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	toḻiloṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkuṅ kālum
	mūṉṟē tiripiṭaṉ oṉṟē iyalpeṉa
	āṅkan nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpē.
itu, mēṟkūṟum puṇarcci puṇarccivakaiyāṉ nāṉkā meṉpatūum, avai colvakaiyāṉ nāṉkā  meṉpatūum, puṇarvatu collum collumēyaṉṟu, eḻuttum eḻuttum eṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - nilaimoḻi varumoḻi yeṉappaṭṭa vaṟṟuḷ, niṟutta colliṉ īṟu āku eḻuttoṭu kuṟittu varu kiḷavi mutal eḻuttu iyaiya - niṟutta colliṉatu īṟākiṉṟa eḻuttiṉōṭu ataṉaik kuṟittu varukiṉṟa colliṉatu mutaleḻuttup porunta, peyaroṭu peyaraip puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu peyarc collaip puṇarkkum kālattum, peyaroṭu toḻilai puṇarkkum kālum - peyarccolloṭu viṉaiccollaip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu peyaraip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉaic colloṭu peyarccollaip puṇarkkum kālattum, toḻiloṭu toḻilaip puṇarkkum kālum - viṉaiccolloṭu viṉaiccollaip puṇarkkum kālattum, tiripu mūṉṟu iyalpu oṉṟu eṉa a nāṉkē moḻipuṇar iyalpu - tiriyum iṭam mūṉṟum iyalpu oṉṟum ākiya an nāṉkē moḻikaḷ tammiṟ puṇarum iyalpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉ kai, cāttaṉ uṇṭāṉ, vantāṉ cāttaṉ, vantāṉ pōyiṉāṉ eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṭaiyum uriyum peyar viṉaikaḷai aṭaintallatu tāmāka nillāmaiyiṉ, peyar viṉaikaṭkē puṇarcci kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(6)
tiripiṉ mūvakai
110.	avaitām
	meypiṟi tātal mikutal kuṉṟaleṉṟu
	ivveṉa moḻipa tiriyu māṟē.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya tiripu mūṉṟum āmāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - at tiriputām, meypiṟitu ātal mikutal kuṉṟal eṉṟu iv eṉa moḻipa tiriyum āṟu - mey piṟitātalum mikutalum kuṉṟalumākiya ivai eṉac colluvar tiriyum neṟiyiṉai e-ṟu.
im mūṉṟum allātatu iyalpeṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	poṟkuṭam, yāṉaikkōṭu, maravēr eṉavarum. kuvaḷai malar eṉpatu iyalpu.
ip puṇarcci nāṉkum oru puṇarccikkaṇṇē nikaḻvu peṟumeṉpatu uraiyiṟkoḷka.
mēl 'iṭam' (puṇar. 6) eṉṟataṉāṉ, tiripu mūṉṟaṉuḷ, yātāyiṉum oṉṟāyatu, oru puṇarccikku iraṇṭum mūṉṟum oruṅku varappeṟum eṉakkoḷka.
e-ṭu:	'makattāṟ koṇṭāṉ' eṉpatu am mikuti iraṇṭu vantatu, 'nīyir kuṟiyir' eṉpatu am mikuti mūṉṟu vantatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(7)
niṟuttacollum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum aṭaiyoṭum tōṉṟutal
111.	niṟutta colluṅ kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum
	aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaik kuriya.
itu, "niṟutta collē kuṟittuvaru kiḷavi" (puṇar. 5) eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) niṟutta collum kuṟittuvaru kiḷaviyum - nilai moḻiyāka niṟuttiṉa collum ataṉaik kuṟittu varucollum, aṭaiyoṭu tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaikku uriya - (tāmē vantu puṇarvataṉṟi), avai yiraṇṭiṉum orōr collaṭaivantu oṉṟit tōṉṟiṉum puṇarnilaikku uriya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṉāyirattoṉṟu, āyirattorupaḵtu,  patiṉāyirat torupaḵtu eṉa varum.
īṇṭu aṭaiyeṉṟatu ummaittokaiyiṉaiyum, iru peyaroṭṭup paṇputtokaiyiṉaiyum eṉa vuṇarka. avaiyallāta tokaikaḷuḷ viṉaittokaiyum, paṇput tokaiyum piḷantu muṭiyāmaiyiṉ, orucol eṉappaṭum. aṉmoḻit tokaiyum taṉakku vēṟu oru muṭipu iṉmaiyiṉ orucol eṉappaṭum. iṉi oḻinta vēṟṟumait tokaiyum uvamait tokaiyum 'taṉṉiṉa muṭittal' eṉṟataṉāṉ īṇṭu orucol eṉappaṭum.
uṇṭacāttaṉ vantāṉ, uṇṭuvantāṉ cāttaṉ eṉpaṉavum avvāṟē orucol eṉappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(8)
marūukkaḷum puṇarkkappaṭutal
112.	maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅkiyal moḻiyum
	uriyavai uḷavē puṇarnilaic cuṭṭē.
iḵtu, ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi marūukkaḷum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) maruviṉ tokuti mayaṅku iyal moḻiyum - (ilakkaṇa vaḻakkēyaṉṟi) marūuttiraḷākiya talaitaṭumāṟāka mayaṅkiṉa iyalpai yuṭaiya ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiṉa marūu vaḻakkum, uriyavai uḷa puṇarnilaic cuṭṭu - uriyaṉa uḷa puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	mīkaṇ, muṉṟil eṉa varum.
'nilai' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ilakkaṇattoṭu poruntiya marūu vaḻakkallā marūu vaḻakkum puṇarkkappaṭum eṉakkoḷka.	(9)
mikkapuṇarcci iruvakaittātal
113.	vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻip puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum
	eḻuttē cāriyai āyiru paṇpiṉ
	oḻukkal valiya puṇaruṅ kālai.
itu, nālvakaip puṇarcciyuḷ mikka puṇarcci ittaṉmait teṉpatūum, an nālvakaip puṇarcciyum vēṟōrāṟṟāṉ iruvakai yāmeṉpatūum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai kuṟitta puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum - vēṟ ṟumaiyatu poruṇmaiyiṉaik kuṟitta puṇarmoḻiyatu nilaimaiyum, vēṟṟumai alvaḻi puṇarmoḻi nilaiyum - vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattup puṇarmoḻiyatu nilaimaiyum, eḻuttu cāriyai a iru paṇpiṉ oḻukkal valiya - (mikka puṇarccikkaṇ) eḻuttu mikutalum cāriyai mikutalumākiya av viraṇṭu ilakkaṇattāṉum naṭāttutalait tamakku valiyāka uṭaiya, puṇarumkālai - avai puṇaruṅkālattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaṅkōṭu, makaviṉkai, viḷakkuṟitu, paṉaiyiṉ kuṟai eṉak kaṇṭukoḷka.
'oḻukkal valiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, eḻuttum cāriyaiyum uṭaṉ peṟutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	avaṟṟukkōṭu, kalattukkuṟai eṉavarum.
alvaḻi muṟkūṟātatu, vēṟṟumaiyallātatu alvaḻiyeṉa vēṇṭaliṉ eṉpatu. muṉṉē 'puṇarmoḻi' eṉṟu vaittu, 'puṇaruṅ kālai' eṉṟataṉāṟ puṇarccikkaṇṇē vēṟṟumai alvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭāvatu allākkāl, vēṟṟumaiyeṉavē paṭumeṉpatu koḷka. īṇṭu 'alvaḻi' yeṉṟatu perumpāṉmaiyum eḻuvāyiṉai.	(10)
vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷiṉ peyarum muṟaiyum tokaiyum
114.	aioṭu kuiṉ atukaṇ ṇeṉṉum
	avvā ṟeṉpa vēṟṟumai yurupē.
itu, mēl vēṟṟumai yeṉṉappaṭṭavaṟṟatu peyarum, muṟaiyum, tokaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṉum - ai oṭu ku iṉ atu kaṇ eṉṟu collappaṭum. a āṟu eṉpa vēṟṟumai urupu - av vāṟum eṉṟu colluvar vēṟṟumaiyurupukaḷai e-ṟu.
iv vāṟum allaṉa vellām alvaḻi eṉappaṭum. avai eḻuvāyum, viḷiyum, uvamattokaiyum, ummaittokaiyum, paṇput tokaiyum, irupeyaroṭṭup paṇputtokaiyum, muṟṟum, iru vakai eccamum, iṭaiyum, uriyum eṉa ivai.	(11)
nāṉkaṉurupum ēḻaṉurupum vaṉkaṇattoṭu puṇarumāṟu
115.	valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku
	olvaḻi oṟṟiṭai mikutal vēṇṭum.
itu, nāṉkāvataṟkum ēḻāvataṟkum urupiyalai nōkkiṟṟoru karuvi uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) valleḻuttu mutaliya vēṟṟumai urupiṟku - valleḻuttu mutalākavuṭaiya vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku, olvaḻi oṟṟu iṭai mikutal vēṇṭum - poruntiṉa iṭattu valloṟṟāyiṉum melloṟṟāyiṉum iṭaikkaṇ mikutal vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ūrkku, nīrkku, ūrkkaṇ, nīrkkaṇ eṉa valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. taṅkaṇ, eṅkaṇ eṉa melleḻuttu mikkaṉa.
'olvaḻi' eṉṟataṉāṉ, aracarkaṇ, pārppārkaṇ eṉa oṟṟu mikātaṉavum koḷka.
'meypiṟi tātal' (puṇar. 7) mutalāya nāṉku puṇarcciyum urupu puṇarccikkaṇṇum eytaliṉ, meypiṟitātalai eṭuttōtātu mikkataṉai eṭuttōtiya vataṉāṉ, mikka puṇarcci yallaṉavum īṇṭē koḷka.
e-ṭu:	poṟku, poṟkaṇ, āṅkaṇ, īṅkaṇ, ūṅkaṇ, avaṟku, ivaṟku; nuṅkaṇ, koṟṟaṟku, cāttaṟku eṉa varum.
avaṉkaṇ, cāttaṉkaṇ eṉpuḻi iyalpum itaṉāṉē koḷka. kuṉṟiya puṇarcci vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(12)
āṟaṉurupiṉ akaram keṭumiṭam
116.	āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi
	īṟā(ku) akaramuṉaik keṭutal vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, āṟāvataṟkut tokaimarapiṉai nōkkiyatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉ akarak kiḷavi - āṟām vēṟṟumaiyākiya atu eṉṉum colliṭattu akaramākiya eḻuttu, īṟu āku akaramuṉai keṭutal vēṇṭum - (neṭumutal kuṟukum) moḻiyīṟṟu uḷatākiṉṟa akarattiṉ muṉṉart tāṉ keṭutal vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	namatu, ematu, taṉatu, eṉatu, niṉatu eṉa varum.
mēl īṟāku akaram itaṟkut tārātu, iv vuru pakaramē ēṟi muṭiya amaitaliṉ, atu kēṭātal vēṇṭā eṉiṉ, neṭumutal kuṟuki vikārappaṭṭu niṉṟa moḻiyākaliṉ ataṉmēl urupakara mēṟi muṭiyal vēṇṭā rāyiṉār pōlu meṉpatu.	(13)
vēṟṟumaiyurupu peyarppiṉṉarp puṇartal
117.	vēṟṟumai vaḻiya peyarpuṇar nilaiyē.
itu, vēṟṟumaiyurupu peyarkkaṇ niṟkumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai peyar vaḻiya puṇarnilai - vēṟṟumai peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉiṭattaṉa avaṟṟoṭu puṇarum nilaimaikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉai, cāttaṉoṭu eṉa varum.
mēl "urupunilai tiriyātu īṟupeyark kākum" (vēṟ. 8) eṉṟāraṉṟō veṉiṉ, peyaroṭu peyar mutaliya nālvakaip puṇarcciyaiyum vēṟṟumai alvaḻiyeṉa iraṇṭāka aṭakkaliṉ, viṉaivaḻiyum urupu varumeṉpatupaṭa niṉṟatākaliṉ īṇṭu itu kūṟappaṭṭatu.	(14)
peyarnilaic cuṭṭu iruvakaippaṭutal
118.	uyartiṇaip peyarē aḵṟiṇaip peyareṉṟu
	āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa peyarnilaic cuṭṭē.
itu, vēṟṟumaiyoṭu puṇarum peyarkaṭkup peyarum, muṟaiyum, tokaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyartiṇaip peyarē aḵṟiṇaip peyar eṉṟu a iraṇṭu eṉpa - uyartiṇaip peyarum aḵṟiṇaip peyarumākiya av viraṇṭumeṉṟu collupa, peyar nilaic cuṭṭu - peyarākiya nilaimai yatu karuttiṉai e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūu, makaṭūu eṉpaṉa uyartiṇaip peyar. oṉṟu pala eṉpaṉa aḵṟiṇaip peyar.
maṟṟu viravuppeyar kūṟārāyatu eṉṉai yeṉiṉ,  maṟṟatu cāttaṉ vantāṉ; vantatu eṉap puṇarccikkaṇ perumpāṉmaiyum oru tiṇaip pāṟ paṭutaliṉ, av viraṇṭāka aṭakkik kūṟiṉā reṉpatu.	(15)
peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉarc cāriyai varutal
119.	avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṟ cāriyai varumē.
itu, cāriyai varum iṭam kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkiṉ cāriyai varum - ap peyarkaḷiṉ piṉṉākiya iṭattiṉkaṇṇē cāriyai varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭūuviṉ kai, makaṭūuviṉ kai, palavaṟṟuk kōṭu eṉa varum.	(16)
cāriyaikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟaiyum
120.	avaitām
	iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē
	oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē
	aṉṉeṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭap piṟavum
	aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyai moḻiyē.
itu, cāriyaikaṭkup peyarum muṟaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - mēṟcāriyai yeṉṉappaṭṭavaitām, iṉṉē vaṟṟē attē ammē oṉṉē āṉē akkē ikkē aṉ eṉ kiḷavi uḷappaṭa piṟavum - iṉṉum vaṟṟum attum ammum oṉṉum āṉum akkum ikkum aṉ eṉṉum collumākiya av voṉpatum uḷappaṭap piṟavum, aṉṉa eṉpa cāriyai moḻi - ac cāriyaiyām taṉmaiya eṉṟu colluvar cāriyaiyākiya moḻikaḷai e-ṟu.
'piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, tam, nam, num, um, keḻu, ē, ai, ñāṉṟu eṉpaṉavuṅ koḷka.
'eṭutta naṟaviṉ kulaiyalaṅ kāntaḷ' (kali. 40) eṉpuḻi alam eṉpatoru cāriyaiyum uṇṭāleṉiṉ, ataṉai alaṅku kāntaḷ eṉpataṉ vikārameṉpa.
iṉcāriyai vaḻakkup payiṟciyāṉum nūluṭ palakālum eṭut tōtappaṭalāṉum, vāḷā ōtiyavaḻit tāṉē cēṟalāṉum muṉvaikkap paṭṭatu. aṉ cāriyaiyum atupōlac ciṟappuṭaimaiyiṉ piṉ vaikkap paṭṭatu. iṭai niṉṟavaṟṟiyalpum aṟintu koḷka.	(17)
iṉcāriyaiyatu mutal tiriyumāṟu
121.	avaṟṟuḷ
	iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟuti
	muṉṉark keṭutal urittu mākum.
itu, avaṟṟuḷ iṉcāriyai mutal tiriyumāṟu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa cāriyaikaḷuḷ, iṉṉiṉ ikaram āviṉ iṟutimuṉṉar - iṉcāriyaiyiṉatu ikaram ā eṉṉum collīṟṟumuṉṉar, keṭutalum urittākum – keṭāmaiyē yaṉṟik keṭutalum urittām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṉai, āviṉai, āṉkōṭu, āviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.
'muṉṉar' eṉṟataṉāṉ, 'mā' eṉṉum colliṉ muṉṉum av viru vitiyum eytum.
e-ṭu:	māṉai, māviṉai, māṉkōṭu, māviṉkōṭu eṉa varum.	(18)
ataṉiṟuti tiriyum iṭam
122.	aḷavāku moḻimutal nilaiiya uyirmicai
	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ ākiya nilaittē.
iḵtu, iṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) aḷavākum moḻi mutal - aḷavup peyarākum moḻi mutaṟkaṇ, nilaiiya uyirmicai ṉaḵkāṉ - nilaipeṟṟa uyirukku mēlāy niṉṟa iṉcāriyaiyatu ṉakāram, ṟaḵkāṉ ākiya nilaittu - ṟakāramākiya nilaimaittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	patiṟṟakal, patiṟṟuḻakku eṉpuḻi avvāṟu varutal aṟika.
'nilaittu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟavaḻiyum iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram ṟakaramātal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	patiṟṟoṉṟu, patiṟṟēḻu eṉa varum.	(19)
vaṟṟuccāriyaiyatu mutal tiriyumāṟu
123.	vaḵkāṉ meykeṭac cuṭṭumutal aimmuṉ
	aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpē.
itu, vaṟṟu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutal aimuṉ - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya aikāra īṟṟuc colmuṉṉar, vaḵkāṉ mey keṭa  aḵkāṉ niṟṟal ākiya paṇpu - vaṟṟuccāriyai taṉ vakaramākiya meykeṭa akaram niṟṟalākiya paṇpiṉaiyuṭaittu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avaiyaṟṟai, ivaiyaṟṟai, uvaiyaṟṟai eṉavum, avaiyaṟṟukkōṭu eṉavum varum.
'ākiya paṇpu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, cuṭṭu mutalākiya ai eṉ iṟuti aikārattoṭu nillātavaḻi, vaṟṟiṉ vakaram akaram niṟkak keṭātu vaṟṟāyē niṟṟal koḷka.
maṟṟitu, "tirintataṉ tiripu piṟitu" eṉṉum nayattāṟ keṭātē niṟkumākaliṉ, itu kūṟavēṇṭā eṉiṉ, "cuṭṭu mutal ai īṟṟuc colliṉ aimuṉ" eṉa ōtātu, "cuṭṭu mutal aimmuṉ" eṉa ac col muṉ ellām keṭuvatupōla ōtiṉamaiyiṉ, vēṇṭiṟṟeṉpatu.	(20)
ṉakaraīṟṟuc cāriyaikaḷatu īṟu tiriyum iṭam
124.	ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ nāṉka ṉurupiṟku.
itu, ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyai nāṉkaṟkum īṟu tiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāṉkaṉ urupiṟku ṉaḵkāṉ ṟaḵkāṉ - nāṉkām urupiṟku (ṉakara īṟṟuc cāriyaiyellām) ṉakaram ṟakaramām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: viḷaviṟku, kōoṟku, orupāṟku, ataṟku eṉa varum.	(21)
āṉcāriyai īṟu tiriyum iṭam
125.	āṉiṉ ṉakaramum ataṉō raṟṟē
	nāḷmuṉ varūum valmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkē.
iḵtu, āṉcāriyai poruṭpuṇarccikkaṇ īṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) nāḷmuṉ varum val mutal toḻiṟku āṉiṉ ṉakaramum - nāṭpeyar muṉṉar varum valleḻuttai mutalākavuṭaiya viṉaic coṟkaṇ varum āṉcāriyaiyiṉ ṉakaramum, ataṉ ōr aṟṟu - an nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇ varum āṉcāriyaiyōṭu oru taṉmaittāy ṉakāram ṟakaramām e-ṟu.
'toḻiṟku' eṉpataṉait 'toḻiṟkaṇ' eṉa mayakkamākak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paraṇiyāṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
ummaiyai iraṭṭuṟa moḻitalāṉē etiratu taḻīiya tākki, ataṉāṉ nāḷallavaṟṟumuṉ varum vaṉmutaṟ ṟoḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaramum ṟakāramāyt tirital koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paṉiyiṟ koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
toḻiṟkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiriyumeṉa, peyarkkaṇ iṉṉiṉ ṉakaram tiritalum tiriyāmaiyumuṭaiya teṉpatu  ñāpakattāṟ koḷḷap paṭum.
e-ṭu:	paṟampiṟ pāri, vaṇṭiṉ kāl eṉa varum.	(22)
attuccāriyaiyatu akaram keṭumiṭam
126.	attiṉ akaram akaramuṉai yillai.
iḵtu, attu mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attiṉ akaram akaramuṉai illai - attuc cāriyaiyiṉ akaram akaravīṟṟuc colmuṉṉar illaiyākum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	makattukkai eṉa varum.	(23)
ikkuc cāriyaiyatu ikaram keṭumiṭam
127.	ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉai yaṟṟē.
iḵtu, ikkuc cāriyai mutal tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ikkiṉ ikaram ikaramuṉai aṟṟu - ikkuc cāriyaiyiṉatu ikaram ikara īṟṟuccol muṉṉar mēṟkūṟiyavāṟu pōlak keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āṭikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(24)
aikāra īṟṟiṉ muṉṉarum av vikaram keṭutal
128.	aiyiṉ muṉṉarum avviyal nilaiyum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) aiyiṉ muṉṉarum a iyal nilaiyum - (ikkiṉ ikaram) ikaravīṟṟuc col muṉṉaṉṟi aikāravīṟṟuc col muṉṉarum mēṟkūṟappaṭṭa keṭutaliyalpilē niṟkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cittiraikkuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.	(25)
akkuc cāriyaiyatu īṟu tiriyum iṭam
129.	eppeyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi
	akkiṉ iṟutimeym micaiyoṭuṅ keṭumē
	kuṟṟiya lukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu.
iḵtu, akkīṟu tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) e peyar muṉṉarum valleḻuttu varuvaḻi akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṟṟat tōṉṟātu - evvakaippaṭṭa peyar muṉṉum valleḻuttu varumiṭattu akkiṉ iṟutik kuṟṟiyalukaram muṭiyat tōṉṟātu, mey micaiyoṭum keṭum - ataṉāṟ paṟṟappaṭṭa valleḻuttākiya mey taṉakku mēl niṉṟa meyyoṭum keṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kuṉṟakkūkai, maṉṟappeṇṇai, īmakkuṭam, aracak kaṉṉi, tamiḻakkūttu eṉavarum.
'muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ vaṉkaṇamaṉṟi maṟṟak kaṇaṅkaṭkum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	tamiḻanūl, tamiḻayāppu, tamiḻavaraiyar eṉa varum.	(26)
am cāriyaiyatu īṟu tiriyumāṟu
130.	ammiṉ iṟuti kacatak kālait
	taṉmey tirintu ṅañana ākum.
itu, ammiṉ iṟuti tiriyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ammiṉ iṟuti ka ca tak kālai - ammiṉ iṟutiyākiya makaravoṟṟu ka ca takkaḷ varumoḻiyāka vanta kālattu, taṉ mey tirintu ṅa ña na ākum - taṉ vaṭivutirintu ṅa ña nakkaḷām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, puḷiyañcetiḷ, puḷiyantōl eṉa varum.
'taṉmey' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ammiṉ iṟuti makaramēyaṉṟit tam nam num um eṉpaṉavaṟṟiṉ iṟuti makaramum tiriyumeṉpatu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellārtaṅkaiyum, ellānaṅkaiyum, ellīr nuṅkaiyum, 'vāṉa varivillun tiṅkaḷum', eṉa varum.	(27)
av īṟu iyalpām iṭam
131.	meṉmaiyum iṭaimaiyum varūum kālai
	iṉmai vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) meṉmaiyum iṭaimaiyum varum kālai - ammiṉ iṟuti meṉmaiyum iṭaimaiyum varumoḻiyāy varuṅkālattu; iṉmai vēṇṭum eṉmaṉār pulavar - iṉṟimuṭitalai vēṇṭum eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiya ñeri; nuṉi, muri, yāḻ, vaṭṭu eṉa  varum.
uraiyiṟ kōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷiyavilai eṉavum uyir varuvaḻik kēṭum koḷka. puḷiyilai eṉa muḻuvatūum kēṭu kūṟalum koḷka.	(28)
iṉ cāriyai muṟṟum keṭum iṭam
132.	iṉṉeṉa varūum vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku
	iṉṉeṉ cāriyai iṉmai vēṇṭum.
iḵtu, iṉ cāriyai muḻuvatūum keṭum iṭam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) iṉ eṉa varum vēṟṟumai urupiṟku - iṉ eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa vēṟṟumai yurupiṟku, iṉ eṉ cāriyai iṉmai vēṇṭum - iṉ eṉṉum cāriyaitāṉ iṉṟi muṭital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉ, palāviṉ eṉa varum.
'avaṟṟuḷ iṉṉiṉ ikaram' (puṇar. 18) eṉṟataṉ piṉ vaiyātataṉāl, ciṟupāṉmai iṉ cāriyai keṭātu niṟṟal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pāmpiṉiṟ kaṭitu tēḷ eṉa varum.	(29)
cāriyaikaṭkellām potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam;
oruvurupu cāriyai peṟutalum peṟāmaiyum uṭaittātal
133.	peyarun toḻilum pirintoruṅ kicaippa
	vēṟṟumai yurupu nilaipeṟu vaḻiyum
	tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutik kaṇṇum
	oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇic
	coṟcitar maruṅkiṉ vaḻivantu viḷaṅkā(tu)
	iṭainiṉ ṟiyaluñ cāriyai iyaṟkai
	uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum.
itu, cāriyaikaṭkellām potuvāyatōr ilakkaṇam uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) peyarum toḻilum pirintu oruṅku icaippa - peyarccollum viṉaiccollum peyarum viṉaiyumāyp pirintum peyarum peyarumāyk kūṭiyum icaippa, vēṟṟumai urupu nilaipeṟu vaḻiyum - vēṟṟumai yurupu tokātu nilaipeṟṟa iṭattiṉum, tōṟṟam vēṇṭāt tokutikkaṇṇum - avai tōṟṟutal vēṇṭāta tokutikkaṇṇum, oṭṭutaṟku oḻukiya vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi - tām tām poruntutaṟkēṟpa naṭanta vaḻakkoṭu porunti, col citar maruṅkiṉ - cāriyai peṟum puṇarmoḻikaḷaip pirittuk kāṇumiṭattu, vaḻi vantu viḷaṅkātu iṭainiṉṟu iyalum - avaṟṟiṉ piṉ vantu viḷaṅkātu avaṟṟiṭaiyē niṉṟu naṭakkum, cāriyai iyaṟkai - cāriyaiyiṉ iyalpu. uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ okkum - avaitām uṇṭātalum illaiyātalum oṭu urupiṉiṭattu okkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaviṉaik kuṟaittāṉ, viḷaviṉaik kuṟaittavaṉ eṉavum, nilāttukkoṇṭāṉ, nilāttuk koṇṭavaṉ eṉavum varum.
oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakkaṉmaiyiṉ, nilākkatir nilāmuṟṟam eṉpaṉa cāriyai peṟāvāyiṉa. ellā nammaiyum eṉac cāriyai īṟṟiṉkaṇṇum varutaliṉ, iṭainiṉṟiyaṟal perumpāṉmai yeṉak koḷka.
pūviṉoṭu virinta kūntaleṉavum, pūvoṭu virinta kūntaleṉa vum, uṭaimaiyum iṉmaiyum oṭuvayiṉ ottavāṟu.
iyaṟkai eṉṟataṉāṉ oṭuvurupiṉkaṇ cāriyai peṟutalum peṟāmaiyum oḻiya, orōvaḻip peṟṟē varumeṉpatu koḷka--
e-ṭu:	palavaṟṟoṭu eṉa varum.	(30)
attuccāriyai vaṟṟuccāriyai varumiṭattu
nilaivarumoḻikaḷ puṇarnilai
134.	attē vaṟṟē āyiru moḻimēl
	oṟṟumey keṭutal teṟṟeṉ ṟaṟṟē
	avaṟṟumuṉ varūum valleḻuttu mikumē.
iḵtu, attum vaṟṟum varumiṭattu nilaimoḻiyiṉum varumoḻiyiṉum varuñ ceykai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) attē vaṟṟē a irumoḻimēl oṟṟu mey keṭutal teṟṟeṉṟaṟṟu - attum vaṟṟumākiya av virumoḻi mēlniṉṟa oṟṟut taṉvaṭivu keṭutal teḷiyappaṭṭatu, avaṟṟu muṉvarum valleḻuttu mikum - av virucāriyai muṉṉum varum valleḻuttu mikum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalattukkuṟai, avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum.
kalaṉ eṉa, ṉakāramāka niṟuttik keṭukka.
'oṉṟiṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, puḷḷiyīṟalvaḻi vikāravakai yāṉ niṉṟaṉavum avaṟṟiṉmicai yoṟṟeṉṟu keṭukkappaṭumeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	avaṟṟukkōṭu eṉa varum. iḵtu aikāra īṟu.
īṇṭu 'valleḻuttu miku'meṉṟatu, īṟṟu valleḻuttiṉṟit tirintu muṭiyum ṉakaramum ṇakaramum lakaramum ḷakaramum eṉa ivaṟṟai nōkkiyeṉa vuṇarka.
attu muṟkūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ, attiṉ micai yoṟṟuk keṭātu niṟkavum peṟum.
e-ṭu:	viṇṇattuk koṭkum, veyilattuc ceṉṟāṉ, iruḷattuk koṇṭāṉ eṉa varum.
'mey' eṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaram piṟavuyiriṉ muṉṉum keṭutal koḷka.
e-ṭu:	aṇṇāttēri eṉavarum.
'teṟṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, attiṉ akaram keṭātu niṟṟalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	viḷavattukkaṇ eṉa varum.	(31)
eḻuttuc cāriyaikaḷiṉ peyarum muṟaiyum
135.	kāramuṅ karamuṅ kāṉoṭu civaṇi
	nērat tōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyai.
iḵtu, eḻuttuc cāriyaikaṭkup peyarum muṟaiyum uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu civaṇi - kāramum karamum kāṉoṭu porunti, nērattōṉṟum eḻuttiṉ cāriyai - ellā āciriyarāṉum uṭaṉpaṭat tōṉṟum eḻuttuccāriyai yātaṟku e-ṟu.
'nērat tōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ, nērattōṉṟātaṉa āṉam, ēṉam ōṉam, eṉa ivai.	(32)
karamum kāramum neṭṭeḻuttup peṟāmai
136.	avaṟṟuḷ
	karamuṅ kāṉum neṭṭeḻut tilavē.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuṭ cila cāriyai cila eḻuttoṭu vārā eṉa eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttu ila - mēṟ collappaṭṭavaṟṟuḷ karamum kāṉum neṭṭeḻuttiṟku ila. kāram neṭṭeḻuttiṟku uṇṭu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ākāram, īkāram eṉa varum.	(33)
kuṟṟeḻuttu muccāriyaiyum peṟutal
137.	varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟuṅ kuṟṟeḻut tuṭaiya.
itu, neṭṭeḻuttiṟkuc cila cāriyai vilakkiṉamaiyiṉ kuṟṟeḻuttiṟkum cila vilakkuvatuṇṭukol eṉṉum aiyam tīrttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) varaṉmuṟai mūṉṟum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭaiya - varalāṟṟu muṟaimaiyaiyuṭaiya mūṉṟu cāriyaiyum kuṟṟeḻuttu uṭaiya e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	akāram, akaram, aḵkāṉ eṉa varum.
'varaṉmuṟai' eṉṟataṉāṉ, aḵkāṉ eṉpuḻi āytamikku muṭiyumeṉpatu koḷka.	(34)
ai, au - iraṇṭum kāṉcāriyaiyum peṟutal
138.	aikāra aukāraṅ kāṉoṭun tōṉṟum.
iḵtu, avaṟṟuḷ kāramuṅ kāṉum eṉpataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aikāram aukāram kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum - neṭ ṭeḻuttukkaḷil aikāramum aukāramum muṉ vilakkappaṭṭa kāṉoṭum tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	aikāṉ, aukāṉ eṉa varum.	(35)
uṭalmēl uyirvan toṉṟutal
139.	puḷḷi yīṟṟumuṉ uyirtaṉit tiyalātu
	meyyoṭuñ civaṇum avviyal keṭuttē.
iḵtu, uyirmutalmoḻi puḷḷiyīṟṟumuṉ varuṅkāṟ piṟappa toru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi īṟṟumuṉ uyir taṉittu iyalātu - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc colmuṉ uyir taṉittu naṭavātu, meyyoṭum civaṇum a iyal keṭuttu - ap puḷḷiyoṭum kūṭum tāṉ taṉiniṉṟa av viyalpiṉaik keṭuttu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	āl + aṭai = ālaṭai eṉa varum.
'oṉṟeṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, iyalpallāta puḷḷimuṉ uyirvantālum iv viti koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ataṉai eṉavum, nāṭuri eṉavum varum.
puḷḷiyīṟṟu muṉṉum eṉa, tokuttu niṉṟa ummaiyai virittata ṉāṉē kuṟṟiyalukarattiṉ muṉṉum iv viti koḷka.
e-ṭu:	nākaritu eṉa varum.	(36)
uyir nīṅkiyaviṭattu mey puḷḷipeṟutal
140.	meyyuyir nīṅkiṉ taṉṉuru vākum.
itu, puṇarcciyiṭattu uyirmey uyir  nīṅkiyavaḻip paṭuva toru viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mey uyir nīṅkiṉ taṉ uru ākum - mey taṉṉoṭu kūṭiya uyir puṇarcciyiṭattu nīṅkiya vaḻit taṉ puḷḷi vaṭivu peṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	ataṉai - ataṉ + ai eṉa varum. 	(37)
uṭampaṭumey tōṉṟum iṭam
141.	ellā moḻikkum uyirvaru vaḻiyē
	uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvukoḷal varaiyār.
iḵtu, uyirīṟu uyirmutaṉ moḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi nikaḻva toru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ellā moḻikkum - mūvakaippaṭṭa moḻikkum, uyir varu vaḻi - uyir mutaṉmoḻi varum iṭattu, uṭampaṭu meyyiṉ uruvu koḷal varaiyār - iṭai uṭampaṭumey vaṭivu kōṭalai nīkkār e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	puḷiyaṅkōṭu, eruvaṅkuḻi, viḷavattukkoṭkum eṉa varum.
'uraiyiṟ kōṭal' eṉpataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumeyyāvaṉa yakaramum vakaramum eṉak koḷka. ikaravīṟum īkāravīṟum aikāravīṟum yakaravuṭam paṭumey koḷvaṉa; allaṉa vellām vakaramey koḷvaṉa.
'oṉṟeṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, vikārappaṭṭa moḻik kaṇṇum uṭampaṭumey koḷka.
e-ṭu:	maravaṭi, āyirutiṇai eṉa varum.
'varaiyār' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṭampaṭumey kōṭal orutalai aṉṟeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	kiḷi aritu, mūṅkā illai eṉa varum.	(38)
puṇarmoḻikaḷ ōcaivēṟupāṭṭāl puṇarcci vēṟupaṭumāṟu
142.	eḻuttō raṉṉa poruḷteri puṇarcci
	icaiyiṉ tirital nilaiiya paṇpē.
iḵtu, eḻuttukkaḷ oṉṟu palavātal kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) eḻuttu ōr aṉṉa poruḷ teri puṇarcci - eḻuttu oru taṉmaiyāṉa poruḷ viḷaṅkiniṟkum puṇarmoḻikaḷ, icaiyiṉ tirital nilaiiya paṇpu - ōcai vēṟṟumaiyāṉ puṇarcci vēṟupaṭutal nilaipeṟṟa paṇpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi, kuṉṟēṟāmā eṉa varum. 	(39)
mēlaiccūttirattiṟkup puṟaṉaṭai
143.	avaitām
	muṉṉap poruḷa puṇarcci vāyiṉ
	iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ilavē.
itu, mēlataṟku oru puṟaṉaṭai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaitām - mēṟcolliya puṇarmoḻikaḷtām, muṉṉap poruḷa - muṉṉattiṉāṉ uṇarum poruṇmaiyai yuṭaiya; puṇarcci vāyiṉ iṉṉa eṉṉum eḻuttukkaṭaṉ ila - avai puṇarcci yiṭattu ittaṉmaiya veṉṉum eḻuttu muṟaimaiyai uṭaiyavalla e-ṟu.
cempoṉpatiṉṟoṭi eṉṟavaḻip poṉṉārāycci uḷ vaḻip poṉṉeṉavum, cempārāycci uḷvaḻic cempeṉavum kuṟippāṉ uṇarappaṭṭatu.
maṟṟu, itaṉmēl 'icaiyiṉ tirital' (eḻut. 142) eṉa aṟiyumāṟu kūṟiṉāraṉṟōveṉiṉ, ōcai eṉṟamaiyāṉ aḵtu oliyeḻuttiṟ keṉavum, "iṉṉa veṉṉu meḻuttuk kaṭaṉila" eṉṟataṉāṉ itu varivaṭiviṟkeṉavuṅ koḷka.	(40)

puṇariyal muṟṟiṟṟu.

5
tokaimarapu
iv vōttu eṉṉa peyarttō veṉiṉ, mēl akattōtti ṉuḷ irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉum virintu muṭivaṉavaṟṟaiyellām tokuttu muṭittaliṉ tokaimarapu eṉappaṭṭatu.
mēl puṇariyaluṭ kūṟiya karuvikaḷāṟ ceykaikūṟum vaḻi, tokkup puṇaruñ ceykai kūṟaliṟ puṇariyalōṭu iyaipuṭait tāyiṟṟu.
varumoḻi kacatapa-kkaḷiṉ muṉṉar mikum melleḻuttukkaḷ
144.	kacatapa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum
	melleḻut tiyaṟkai colliya muṟaiyāṉ
	ṅañanama veṉṉum oṟṟā kummē
	aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayi ṉāṉa.
it talaic cūttiram eṉ nutaliṟṟōveṉiṉ, alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyumākiya iruvaḻikkaṇṇum, uyirmayaṅkiyalaiyum (218), puḷḷimayaṅkiyalaiyum (301, 315) nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ka ca ta pa mutaliya moḻimēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttu iyaṟkai - uyirīṟṟiṉum puḷḷiyīṟṟiṉum iru vaḻiyum ka ca ta pakkaḷai mutalākavuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṉmēl tōṉṟum melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpukūṟiṉ, colliya muṟaiyāṉ ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum - melleḻuttu mēṟcollum muṟaimaiyāṉ ka ca ta pakkaḷukku niraṉiṟai vakaiyāṉē ṅa ña na ma veṉṉum oṟṟākum, aṉṉa marapiṉ moḻivayiṉ āṉa - at taṉmaittākiya muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya moḻikaḷiṭattu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷaṅkōṭu; cetiḷ, tōl, pū eṉa varum.
'tōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ tōṉṟi niṉṟaṉavum avvāṟē tirintu melleḻuttā meṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	maraṅkuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa varum.
aṉṉamarapiṉ moḻiyaṉmaiyiṉ, viḷakkuṟumai eṉpuḻi melleḻuttu mikātāyiṟṟu.	(1)
iruvaḻiyum vaṉkaṇam oḻintakaṇaṅkaḷ iyalpāy muṭital
145.	ñanama yaveṉum mutalāku moḻiyum
	uyirmuta lākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa
	aṉṟi yaṉaittum ellā vaḻiyum
	niṉṟa coṉmuṉ iyalpā kummē.
iḵtu, irupattunāṉku īṟṟiṉ muṉṉum, vaṉkaṇamoḻinta kaṇaṅkaṭku iruvaḻiyum varumoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ña na ma ya va eṉum mutal āku moḻiyum - ña na ma ya va eṉṟu collappaṭum mutaleḻuttu uḷavākum moḻiyum, uyir mutalākiya moḻiyum uḷappaṭa - uyirmutalākiya moḻiyumāka, aṉṟi aṉaittum ellā vaḻiyum - av vaṉaittu moḻiyum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyumākiya ellāviṭattum, niṉṟa colmuṉ iyalpākum - irupattunāṉku īṟṟup peyarākiya nilaimoḻi muṉṉar iyalpāki muṭiyum e-ṟu.
īṇṭu 'uḷappaṭa'veṉpatu āka veṉṉum poruṇmaittu.
e-ṭu:	viḷa, tāḻ eṉa niṟutti ñāṉṟatu, nīṇṭatu, māṇṭatu, yātu, valitu, aṭaintatu, āṭiṟṟu, iṭintatu, īriṟṟu, uṭaintatu, ūṟiṟṟu, eḻuntatu, ēṟiṟṟu, aitu, oṭintatu, ōṭiṟṟu, auviyattatu, nuntaiyatu eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci, yāppu, valimai, aṭaivu, āṭṭam, iṭipu,  īṭṭam, uṭaipu, ūṟṟam, eḻu, ēṇi, aiyam, oṭukkam, ōkkam, auviyam, nuntai eṉavum oṭṭikkoḷka.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, oṟṟiraṭṭalum, uṭampaṭumey kōṭalum, uyirēṟi muṭitalum eṉavarum ikkaruvittiripu mūṉṟum tiripu aṉmaiyiṉ tiripeṉap paṭāveṉpatu koḷka. iḵtu irupattu nāṉku īṟṟiṟkum, alvaḻiyiṉum, vēṟṟumaiyiṉum, akattōttiṉuḷ nāṟpatteṭṭuc cūttirattāṉ muṭivataṉai, īṇṭu oru cūttirattāṉ tokuttu muṭittatāyiṟṟu. mēlum ivvāṟē tokuttu muṭikkiṉṟa vāṟu aṟika.	(2)
ammuṭipu cilavaṟṟiṟku ēlāmai
146.	avaṟṟuḷ 
	melleḻut tiyaṟkai yuṟaḻiṉum varaiyār
	colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟuti yāṉa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya muṭipiṟ cilavaṟṟiṟku, am muṭipu vilakkip piṟitu viti eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa mūṉṟu kaṇattiṉum, melleḻuttu iyaṟkai uṟaḻiṉum varaiyār - melleḻuttiṉatu iyalpu iyalpātalē yaṉṟi uṟaḻntu muṭiyiṉum nīkkār, colliya toṭarmoḻi iṟutiyāṉa - collappaṭṭa toṭar moḻi īṟṟukkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	katirñeri, katirññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉa varum.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, ōreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum, īreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku uṟaḻcci muṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	pūñeri, pūññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum; kāyñeri, kāyññeri; nuṉi, muri eṉavum varum.
'colliya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, ōreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum īreḻut torumoḻiyuḷḷum cilavaṟṟiṟku mikku muṭital koḷka.
eṅiṭu:	kaiññerittār; nīṭṭiṉār, *muṟittār eṉavum, meyñ ñāṉam, meynnūl, meymmaṟantār eṉavum varum.(3)
*(pāṭam) 'maṟittār' eṉpatu.
yāvum ñāvum viṉaiyāka varuvaḻi orutaṉmaiyavām iṭam
147.	ṇaṉaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ yāvum ñāvum
	viṉaiyō raṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
itu, yakara ñakaraṅkaḷ mutalāmvaḻi nikaḻvatoru karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ṉa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ṉa eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷikaḷiṉmuṉ, yāvum ñāvum viṉai ōr aṉaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar - yāvum ñāvum viṉaiccoṟkaṇ mutalātaṟku oru taṉmaiya veṉṟu colluvar *pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇyātta maṇñātta eṉavum, poṉyātta  poṉñātta eṉavum varum.
ñā muṟkūṟātu yā muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, yā mutaṉ moḻikkaṇ ñā varumeṉṟu koḷka.	(4)
*(pāṭam) 'āciriyar' eṉpatu.
ṇakara ṉakara īṟukaḷ alvaḻikkaṇ tiriyāmai
148.	moḻimuta lākum ellā eḻuttum
	varuvaḻi niṉṟa āyiru puḷḷiyum
	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻit tiripiṭaṉ ilavē.
itu, ṇakāravīṟṟiṟkum ṉakāravīṟṟiṟkum alvaḻikkaṇ nilai moḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) moḻimutal ākum ellā eḻuttum varuvaḻi - moḻikku mutalāmeṉṟu collappaṭṭa irupattiraṇṭeḻuttum varumiṭattu, niṉṟa a iru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumai alvaḻi tiripu iṭaṉ ila - *mēṟ colliniṉṟa ṇa ṉakkaḷākiya avviru puḷḷiyum vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattut tiriyum iṭam ila e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, kaṭitu, ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa vaṉkaṇattōṭu oṭṭuka. piṟa kaṇattukkaṇṇum avvāṟē oṭṭuka.
varumoḻi muṟkūṟiya vataṉāṉ, ṇakārattiṟkuc ciṟupāṉmai tiripum uṇṭeṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	cāṭkōl eṉa varum.	(5)
*(pāṭam) 'peyarc colliṉiṉṟa' eṉpatu.
vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum iyalpukaṇam varuvaḻi
avvīṟukaḷ tiriyāmai
149.	vēṟṟumaik kaṇṇum valleḻut talvaḻi
	mēṟkū ṟiyaṟkai yāvayi ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, av viraṇṭu īṟṟiṟkum vēṟṟumaikkaṇ nilaimoḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumaikkaṇṇum - vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarccik kaṇṇum, valleḻuttu alvaḻi - valleḻuttu allāta viṭattu, mēlkūṟu iyaṟkai āvayiṉ āṉa - mēṟkūṟiya iyalpu muṭipām av viraṇṭu puḷḷiyiṭattum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	maṇ, poṉ eṉa niṟutti, ñāṟci, nīṭci eṉa vaṉkaṇam oḻittu ellāvaṟṟōṭum oṭṭuka.	(6)
lakara ṉakara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉart takara nakara varumoḻikaḷ tiriyumāṟu
150.	laṉaveṉa varūum puḷḷi muṉṉar
	tana eṉavariṟ ṟaṉavā kummē.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatoru varumoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) la ṉa eṉa varum puḷḷi muṉṉar - la ṉa eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar, ta na eṉa variṉ - ta na eṉa varumoḻi variṉ, ṟa ṉa ākum - at takara nakaraṅkaḷ niraṉiṟaiyāṉē ṟakara ṉakaraṅkaḷām e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kaḵṟītu, kaṉṉaṉṟu, poṉṟītu, poṉṉaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(7)
ṇakara ḷakara īṟṟiṉ muṉṉar avai tiriyumāṟu
151.	ṇaḷaveṉ puḷḷimuṉ ṭaṇaveṉat tōṉṟum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) ṇa ḷa eṉ puḷḷimuṉ - ṇa ḷa eṉṉum puḷḷikaḷiṉ muṉṉar (atikārattiṉāṉ takāra nakāraṅkaḷ variṉ), ṭa ṇa eṉat tōṉṟum - avai niraṉiṟaiyāṉ ṭakāra ṇakaraṅkaḷāyt tōṉṟum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: 	maṇṭītu, maṇṇaṉṟu, muḵṭītu, muṇṇaṉṟu eṉa varum.	(8)
muṉṉilai viṉaiccol vaṉkaṇam varuvaḻi muṭiyumāṟu
152.	uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum
	iyalpā kunavum uṟaḻpā kunavumeṉṟu
	āyī riyala valleḻuttu variṉē.
itu, muṉṉilai viṉaiccol vaṉkaṇattukkaṇ muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum puḷḷi iṟuti muṉṉilaik kiḷaviyum - uyir īṟākiya muṉṉilaic coṟkaḷum puḷḷi iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya muṉṉilaic coṟkaḷum, valleḻuttu variṉ - valleḻuttu mutalmoḻi variṉ, iyalpu ākunavum uṟaḻpu ākunavum eṉṟu a īr iyala - iyalpāvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāvaṉavum eṉa av viraṇṭu iyalpiṉaiyuṭaiya e-ṟu.
uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu eṉṟamaiyāṉ, muṉṉilai viṉaiccol eṉpatu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	eṟikoṟṟā, koṇākoṟṟā; cāttā, tēvā, pūtā eṉavum; uṇkoṟṟā, tiṉkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivai iyalpu. 
	naṭakoṟṟā, naṭakkoṟṟā eṉavum, īrkoṟṟā, īrkkoṟṟā eṉavum varum. ivai uṟaḻcci.	(9)
mēlaimuṭipu cila īṟukaṭku ēlāmai
153.	auveṉa varūum uyiriṟu collum
	ñanamava veṉṉum puḷḷi yiṟutiyum
	kuṟṟiya lukarat tiṟutiyum uḷappaṭa
	muṟṟat tōṉṟā muṉṉilai moḻikkē.
itu, mēl muṭipu kūṟiyavaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku am muṭipu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) au eṉa varum uyir iṟu collum - au eṉa varukiṉṟa uyirīṟṟuc collum, ña na ma va eṉṉum puḷḷiiṟutiyum - ña na ma va eṉṟu collappaṭum puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum, kuṟṟiyalukarattu iṟuti yum uḷappaṭa - kuṟṟiyalukaramākiya iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya collumākiya ivai, muṉṉilai moḻikku muṟṟat tōṉṟā - muṉṉilai moḻikkuk kūṟiya iyalpum uṟaḻvumākiya muṭiviṟku muṟṟattōṉṟā e-ṟu.
'muṟṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, īṇṭu vilakkappaṭṭavaṟṟuṭ kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu oḻittu, oḻintaṉavellām nilaimoḻi ukaram peṟṟu, varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitalum, kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu varumoḻi valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭitaluṅ koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kauvukoṟṟā, kauvukkoṟṟā, uriñukoṟṟā, uriñuk koṟṟā, porunukoṟṟā, porunukkoṟṟā, tirumukoṟṟā, tirumukkoṟṟā, tevvukoṟṟā, tevvukkoṟṟā eṉavum; kūṭṭukoṟṟā, kūṭṭuk koṟṟā eṉavum varum. 	(10)
uyartiṇaippeyar nāṉku kaṇattum iruvaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu
154.	uyirī ṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarum
	puḷḷi yiṟuti uyartiṇaip peyarum
	ellā vaḻiyum iyalpeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, uyartiṇaip peyar nāṉku kaṇattukkaṇṇum, iru vaḻiyum muṭiyumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarum - uyir īṟākiya uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷum, puḷḷi iṟuti uyartiṇaip peyarum - puḷḷi iṟutiyaiyuṭaiya uyartiṇaip peyarkaḷum, ellā vaḻiyum iyalpu eṉa moḻipa - nāṉku kaṇattilum alvaḻiyum vēṟṟumaiyumākiya ellāviṭattum iyalpām eṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nampi eṉavum avaṉ eṉavum niṟutti, alvaḻikkaṇ kuṟiyaṉ, ciṟiyaṉ, tīyaṉ, periyaṉ eṉavum; ñāṉṟāṉ, nīṇṭāṉ, māṇṭāṉ eṉavum; yāvaṉ, valiyaṉ eṉavum; aṭaintāṉ, auviyattāṉ eṉavum; vēṟṟumaikkaṇ kai, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum; ñāṟci, nīṭci, māṭci eṉavum; yāppu, valimai eṉavum; aṭaipu, auviyam eṉavum oṭṭuka. oruveṉ eṉa niṟutti, kuṟiyeṉ, ciṟiyeṉ eṉavum; kai, cevi, talai, puṟam eṉavum taṉmaip peyarkkaṇṇum oṭṭuka.
'uyirīṟu', 'puḷḷiyiṟuti' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇaip poruḷ iyalpaṉṟi muṭivaṉa vellām koḷka.
e-ṭu:	palcāṉṟār, kapilaparaṇar, iṟaivaneṭuvēṭṭuvar, maruttuva māṇikkar, pallaracar eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu muṭintaṉa. kōlikakkaruvi, vaṇṇārappeṇṭir, ācīvakap paḷḷi eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu mikkaṉa. kumarakōṭṭam, kumarakkōṭṭam, pirama kōṭṭam, piramak kōṭṭam eṉa ivai īṟukeṭṭu valleḻuttu uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'ellām' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uyartiṇai(viṉai)ccol iyalpāyum tirintum muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu: uṇpa cāṉṟār, uṇṭār cāṉṟār, uṇpēṉ pārppēṉ eṉa ivai iyalpu. *uṇṭēñ cāṉṟēm, uṇṭēnām eṉa ivai tirintaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(11)
*(pāṭam) 'uṇṭaṇem cāṉṟēm' eṉpatu.
ikaraīṟ ṟuyartiṇaip peyar mikka tiripu peṟutal
155.	avaṟṟuḷ,
	ikaraīṟ ṟuppeyar tiripiṭa ṉuṭaittē.
iḵtu, uyartiṇaip peyaruṭ cilavaṟṟiṟku eytātatu eytuvittal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) avaṟṟuḷ - mēṟcollappaṭṭa uyartiṇaip peyaruḷ, ikara īṟṟup peyar tiripu iṭaṉ uṭaittu - ikaramākiya iṟutiyai yuṭaiya peyar tirintu muṭiyum iṭamum uṭaittu e-ṟu.
uraiyiṟkōṭal eṉpataṉāṉ, itu mikka tiripeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	eṭṭippū, eṭṭippuravu; kāvitippū, kāvitippuravu; nampippū, nampippēṟu eṉa varum.
'iṭaṉuṭaittu' eṉpataṉāṉ, ikara īṟaṉṟip piṟa īṟum īṟu tiriyātu valleḻuttu mikku varuvaṉa īṇṭukkoḷka. 
e-ṭu: tiṉaippū, tiṉaippuravu eṉa varum.	(12)
viravuppeyar muṭiyumāṟu
156.	aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar iyalpumā ruḷavē.
itu, viravuppeyar muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar iyalpum uḷa - uyar tiṇai yōṭu aḵṟiṇai viravum viravuppeyar iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum uḷa; iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉavum uḷa e-ṟu.
iyalpiṉṟi muṭivaṉa iṉṉavāṟu muṭiyumeṉa, mēl (348-52) akattōttiṉuṭ kūṟappaṭum.
e-ṭu:	cāttaṉkuṟiyaṉ, cāttaṉkuṟitu eṉavum; cāttaṉkai eṉavum; ivvāṟu alvaḻiyiṉum vēṟṟumaiyiṉum nāṉku kaṇattōṭum oṭṭikkoḷka.	(13)
mūṉṟām vēṟṟumait tiripu
157.	puḷḷi yiṟutiyum uyiriṟu kiḷaviyum
	valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟaiyāṉ
	tammi ṉākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ
	meymmai yākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	ammuṟai iraṇṭum uriyavai uḷavē
	vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṟ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum.
itu, mūṉṟām vēṟṟumait tiripu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu. 
(i-ḷ.) puḷḷi iṟutiyum uyir iṟu kiḷaviyum - puḷḷiyīṟṟuc collum uyirīṟṟuc collum, valleḻuttu mikuti colliya muṟaiyāṉ - valleḻuttiṉatu mikuti mēṟcollum muṟaimaiyāṉ, tammiṉ ākiya toḻiṟcol muṉvariṉ - am mūṉṟāvataṉ poru ḷākiya viṉaimutaṟ poruḷkaḷ tammāṉ uḷavākiya viṉaic coṟkaḷ tām avaṟṟumuṉvariṉ, meymmaiyākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum a muṟai iraṇṭum uriyavai uḷa - iyalpākalum uṟaḻat tōṉṟutalu mākiya am muṟaimaiyiṉaiyuṭaiya iraṇṭu ceykaiyum uriyaṉa uḷa, vēṟṟumai maruṅkiṉ pōṟṟal vēṇṭum - avaṟṟai mēlē vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipu collumiṭattup pōṟṟiyaṟital vēṇṭum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	nāykōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pulikōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cārappaṭṭāṉ, tīṇṭappaṭṭāṉ, pāyappaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivai iyalpu. cūrkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, cūrkkōṭpaṭṭāṉ, vaḷikōṭ paṭṭāṉ, vaḷikkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci.
'puḷḷiyīṟu uyirīṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, pēeykōṭpaṭṭāṉ pēeyk kōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ ekarappēṟu koḷka. 'uriyavai yuḷa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, pāmpukōṭpaṭṭāṉ, pāppukkōṭpaṭṭāṉ eṉṉum uṟaḻcciyuḷ nilaimoḻiyoṟṟut tiriyāmaiyum tiritalum koḷka.	(14)
iraṇṭām vēṟṟumait tiripu
158.	melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum
	iyaṟkai maruṅkiṉ mikaṟkai tōṉṟalum
	uyirmika varuvaḻi yuyirkeṭa varutalum
	cāriyai yuḷvaḻic cāriyai keṭutalum
	cāriyai yuḷvaḻit taṉṉurupu nilaiyalum
	cāriyai yiyaṟkai uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum
	uyartiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum
	aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyark kavviya ṉilaiyalum
	meypiṟi tākiṭat tiyaṟkai yātalum
	aṉṉa piṟavun taṉṉiyal maruṅkiṉ
	meypeṟak kiḷantu poruḷvarain ticaikkum
	aikāra vēṟṟumait tiripeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, iraṇṭām vēṟṟumaittiripu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi valippoṭu tōṉṟalum - melleḻuttu mikumiṭattu valleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟutalum, valleḻuttu mikuvaḻi melippoṭu tōṉṟalum - valleḻuttu mikumiṭattu melleḻuttātaloṭu tōṉṟutalum, iyaṟkai maruṅkiṉ mikaṟkai tōṉṟalum - iyaṟkaiyiṭattu mikuti tōṉṟutalum, uyir mika varuvaḻi uyir keṭa varutalum - uyir mika varumiṭattu av vuyir keṭa varutalum, cāriyai uḷvaḻi cāriyai keṭutalum - cāriyaiyuḷḷa iṭattuc cāriyai keṭutalum, cāriyai uḷvaḻi taṉ urupu nilaiyalum - cāriyai uḷḷaviṭattuc cāriyaiyoṭu taṉṉurupu niṟṟalum, cāriyai iyaṟkai uṟaḻat tōṉṟalum - cāriyai peṟuka eṉṟavaḻic cāriyai peṟātu iyal pākiya moḻikaḷ varumoḻiyiṉum nilaimoḻiyiṉum mikkum tirintum varum varumoḻi valleḻuttukkaḷ uṟaḻcciyākat tōṉṟuta lum, uyartiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu varutalum - uyartiṇaip peyariṭattut taṉṉurupu tokātē virintu varutalum, aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyarkku a iyal nilaiyalum - uyartiṇaiyōṭu aḵṟiṇai viravumpeyarkkum av vurupu av viyalpilē niṟṟalum, meypiṟitu āku iṭattu iyaṟkai ātalum - mey piṟitāy muṭiyumiṭattu iyalpāy muṭitalum, aṉṉa piṟavum - attaṉmaiyaṉa piṟa muṭipukaḷum, taṉ iyal maruṅkiṉ meypeṟa kiḷantu poruḷ varaintu icaikkum - taṉakku iyalpākiya kūṟṟāṉ akattōttiṉuṭ poruḷ peṟa eṭuttōtappaṭṭa vēṟṟumaip poruṭ puṇarcciyatu potu muṭiviṉait tāṉ varaintu vēṟu muṭipiṟṟāy niṉṟu olikkum, aikāra vēṟṟumait tiripu eṉa moḻipa - aikāra vēṟṟumaiyiṉatu vēṟupāṭṭup puṇarcci eṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷakkuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu melleḻuttu mikuvaḻi (218) valleḻuttumikkatu. maraṅkuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu val leḻuttu mikuvaḻi (311) melleḻuttu mikkatu. tāyk kolai eṉpatu iyalpāmiṭattu (359) mikkatu. palāk kuṟaittāṉ eṉpatu uyirmika varuvaḻi (227) uyir keṭṭatu. vaṇṭukoṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyai uḷvaḻic (421) cāriyai keṭṭatu. vaṇṭiṉaik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu cāriyai uḷvaḻit (421) taṉṉurupu nilaiyiṟṟu. puḷi kuṟaittāṉ, puḷikkuṟaittāṉ, 1pūlkuṟaittāṉ, pūṟkuṟaittāṉ eṉa ivai (245, 376) cāriyai yiyaṟkai uṟaḻat tōṉṟiṉa. nampiyaik koṇarntāṉ (157) eṉpatu uyartiṇai maruṅkiṉ oḻiyātu vantatu. koṟṟaṉaik koṇarntāṉ eṉpatu viravuppeyarkku av viyal nilaiyatu. maṇkoṇarntāṉ eṉpatu meypiṟitākiṭattu (303) iyaṟkaiyāy vantatu.
'aṉṉa piṟavum' eṉṟataṉāṟ koḷvaṉa: kaḻikuṟaittāṉ, 2tiṉaipiḷantāṉ eṉpaṉa. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'oḻiyātu' eṉṟataṉāṉ orōvaḻi oḻintum varum.
e-ṭu:	'avaṟkaṇṭu' (akam. 48) eṉavum, 'oṉṉārt teṟalum' (kuṟaḷ. 264) eṉavum ivai uyartiṇaiyuḷ oḻintu vantaṉa. makaṟpeṟṟāṉ, makaṭpeṟṟāṉ eṉa ivai viravup peyaruḷ oḻintu vantaṉa.
iv vilēcutaṉṉāṉē, ivaṟṟiṉ muṭipu vēṟṟumaiyum koḷka.
'meypeṟa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	maikoṇarntāṉ, maikkoṇarntāṉ eṉavum; vilkōḷ, viṟkōḷ eṉavum varum.
ivvāṟu tirintu muṭiyātu, akattōttiṉ potumuṭipē (255) muṭipāyk kaṭukkuṟaittāṉ, ceppuk koṇarntāṉ eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉa muṭivaṉa aṟintu koḷka.
ivvāṟu vēṟupaṭa muṭivatu perumpāṉmaiyum iraṇṭāvatu viṉaiyoṭu muṭiyumvaḻip pōlum. atu "tammiṉākiya toḻiṟ coṉ muṉvariṉ" (tokaimarapu 14) eṉa niṉṟa atikārattāṟ koḷḷa vaittār pōlum.
'taṉṉiṉa muṭittal' eṉpataṉāṉ, ēḻām vēṟṟumai viṉai yoṭu muṭiyumvaḻi iyalpum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'varaipāy varuṭai' (malaipaṭu. 503), 'pulampuk kaṉaṉē pullaṇaṟ kāḷai' (puṟam. 258) eṉa varum.	(15)
1(pāṭam) 'ālkuṟaittāṉ' 'āṟkuṟaittāṉ' eṉpatu.
2(pāṭam)  'paṉaipiḷantāṉ' eṉpatu.
ikara aikāra īṟṟuppeyarkaṭku alvaḻi muṭipu
159.	vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi i ai yeṉṉum
	īṟṟuppeyark kiḷavi mūvakai nilaiiya
	avaitām
	iyalpā kunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum
	uṟaḻā kunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar.
iḵtu, ikaravīṟṟup peyarkkum aikāravīṟṟup peyarkkum alvaḻi muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟṟumai alvaḻi i ai eṉṉum īṟṟup peyark kiḷavi mūvakai nilaiya - vēṟṟumaiyallāta alvaḻiyiṭattu i ai eṉṉum īṟṟaiyuṭaiya peyarccoṟkaḷ mūvakai muṭipu nilaimai yuṭaiya, avaitām iyalpākunavum valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟa ḻākunavum eṉmaṉār pulavar - avaitām iyalpāy muṭivaṉavum val leḻuttu mikuvaṉavum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum ivaiyeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	parutti kuṟitu, 1arai kuṟitu; ciṟitu, tītu, peritu eṉa ivai iyalpu. alikkoṟṟaṉ, 2pulaikkoṟṟaṉ eṉa ivai mikuti. kiḷikuṟitu, kiḷikkuṟitu, tiṉaikuṟitu, tiṉaikkuṟitu eṉa ivai uṟaḻcci.
'peyarkkiḷavi mūvakai nilaiya' eṉavē, peyarkkiḷavi yallāta kiḷavi mikutiyum iyalpum eṉa iruvakaiya eṉak koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ollaik koṇṭāṉ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu viṉaiccol mikuti. ikaravīṟṟu mikuti vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. "tillaic collē" (col. 253) "maṉṉaic collē" (col. 252) eṉṟatu aikāravīṟ ṟiṭaiccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. kaṭikā eṉpatu ikaravīṟṟu uriccolliyalpu. iv vīṟṟumikuti vanta vaḻik kaṇṭukoḷka. paṇaittōḷ eṉpatu aikāravīṟṟu uriccol mikuti. ivaṟṟiyalpu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.	(16)
1(pāṭam) 'karai kuṟitu' eṉpatu.
2(pāṭam) 'pulikkoṟṟaṉ' eṉpatu.
avvīrīṟṟu iṭaiccol cilavaṟṟatu muṭipu
160.	cuṭṭumuta lākiya ikara iṟutiyum
	ekaramutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum
	cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	yāveṉ viṉāviṉ aiyeṉ iṟutiyum
	valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻā kunavum
	colliya maruṅkiṉ uḷaveṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, av vikara aikāra vīṟṟuḷ ēḻām vēṟṟumai iṭap poruḷ uṇaraniṉṟa iṭaiccol muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) cuṭṭu mutalākiya ikara iṟutiyum - cuṭṭeḻuttiṉai mutalākavuṭaiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, ekara mutal viṉāviṉ ikara iṟutiyum - ekaramākiya moḻimutal viṉāviṉuṭaiya ikaravīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, cuṭṭucciṉai nīṭiya aieṉ iṟutiyum - cuṭṭākiya uṟuppeḻuttu nīṇṭa aikāra vīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, yā eṉ viṉāviṉ ai eṉ iṟutiyum - yā eṉṉum viṉāviṉai mutaṟkaṇṇuṭaiya aikāra vīṟṟu iṭaiccollum, valleḻuttu mikunavum uṟaḻākunavum colliya maruṅkiṉ uḷa eṉa moḻipa - valleḻuttu mikku muṭivaṉa vum uṟaḻcciyāy muṭivaṉavum mēṟcollappaṭṭa iṭattiṉ kaṇṇē uḷaveṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	atōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, itōḷikkoṇṭāṉ, utōḷik koṇṭāṉ, etōḷikkoṇṭāṉ; ceṉṟāṉ, tantāṉ, pōyiṉāṉ eṉavum;
	āṇṭaikkoṇṭāṉ, īṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ, ūṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ, yāṇṭaik koṇṭāṉ eṉavum ivai mikku muṭintaṉa.
avvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, avvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ; ivvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, ivvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; uvvaḻi koṇṭāṉ, uvvaḻik koṇṭāṉ; evvaḻikoṇṭāṉ, evvaḻikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum, āṅkavaikoṇṭāṉ, āṅkavaikkoṇṭāṉ; īṅkivai koṇṭāṉ, īṅkivaikkoṇṭāṉ eṉavum; ūṅkuvaikoṇṭāṉ, ūṅkuvaik koṇṭāṉ yāṅkavai koṇṭāṉ, yāṅkavaik koṇṭāṉ  eṉavum ivai uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ aikāravīṟṟuḷ uṟaḻntu muṭintaṉa tiripuṭaiyaṉa. tiripillaṉa peṟṟavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
'colliya maruṅku' eṉṟataṉāṉ, piṟa aikāravīṟu mikku muṭivaṉa koḷka.
e-ṭu:	paṇṭaic cāṉṟār, orutiṅkaḷaik kuḻavi eṉa varum.	(17)
neṭṭeḻuttiṉ muṉṉarniṉṟa oṟṟukkeṭutalum, kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟu iraṭṭutalum
161.	neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟumey keṭutalum
	kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuru viraṭṭalum
	aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟiyiya leṉpa.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatoru nilaimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu mey keṭutalum - neṭ ṭeḻuttiṉmuṉ niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu keṭutalum, kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar taṉ uruvu iraṭṭalum - kuṟṟeḻuttiṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟut taṉ vaṭivu iraṭṭutalum, aṟiyat tōṉṟiya neṟi iyal eṉpa - ivai aṟiyumpaṭi tōṉṟiya muṟaimaiyāṉa iyalpuṭaiyaṉaveṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kōṟītu, kōṉaṉṟu eṉa ivai neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟuk keṭṭaṉa. 
	maṇṇakal, poṉṉakal eṉa ivai kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉart taṉṉuruvu iraṭṭiṉa.
mēlaiccūttirattu (162) āṟaṉurupu muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, oṟṟu iraṭṭutal uyirmutaṉmoḻik kaṇṇateṉṟu koḷka.
kuṟiyatu piṉkūṟiya muṟaiyaṉṟik kūṟṟiṉāṉ neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟa vaḻiyum kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāy iraṭṭutalum, kuṟiyatu tirintu neṭiyatāyavaḻi ataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟāyk keṭutalum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	tammai, nammai eṉa ivai neṭiyaṉa kuṟukiniṉṟu oṟṟu iraṭṭiṉa. maṟṟaiyatu vantavaḻik kaṇṭu koḷka.
'aṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, neṭiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟukkeṭuvatu takāra nakāraṅkaḷ vantu tirintavaḻi yeṉpatūum, āṇṭu ellām keṭā teṉpatūum koḷka. tēṉṟītu eṉpatu āṇṭuk keṭātatu.
'neṟiyiyal' eṉṟataṉāṟ kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar niṉṟa oṟṟiṉṟip puṇarcciyāṟ peṟṟatum iraṭṭumeṉa vuṇarka. 
e-ṭu:	avvaṭai eṉavarum.	(18)
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉar oṟṟu iraṭṭā iṭam
162.	āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉum
	kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟ ṟiraṭṭa lillai
	īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyum
	neṭumutal kuṟuku moḻimuṉ ṉāṉa.
iḵtu, urupiyalai nōkkiyatoru nilaimoḻik karuvi kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) āṟaṉ urupiṉum nāṉkaṉ urupiṉum īṟu āku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyum - āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉka ṉurupiṉkaṇṇum īṟāku puḷḷikaḷ akarattoṭu nilaipeṟum, neṭumutal kuṟukum moḻi muṉ kūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal illai - neṭitākiya mutaleḻuttuk kuṟuki muṭiyum moḻikkaṇ mēṟkūṟiya kuṟṟoṟṟu iraṭṭal āṟaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum nāṉkaṉurupiṉkaṇṇum illai e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	tamatu, tamakku; namatu, namakku eṉavarum.
'kūṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉē neṭumutal kuṟukāta moḻikku iv viru vitiyum koḷka.
e-ṭu:	ellār tamatum, ellār tamakkum eṉa varum.	(19)
ṉanumṉa iṟuti oṟṟum aṟṟātal
163.	nummeṉ iṟutiyum annilai tiriyātu.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) num eṉ iṟutiyum a nilai tiriyātu - num eṉṉum makaraviṟuti mēṟkūṟiya īṟāku puḷḷi akaramoṭu nilaiyalum kuṟṟoṟṟiraṭṭāmaiyumākiya annilaimaiyiṉ tiriyātu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	numakku, numatu eṉa varum.	 (20)
yakaramum uyirum varumiṭattu iyalpāka niṟkum puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷ
164.	ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi yiṟuti
	yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟkai.
itu, puḷḷimayaṅkiyalai nōkkiyatoru nilaimoḻic ceykai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) ukaramoṭu puṇarum puḷḷi iṟuti - ukarap pēṟṟoṭu puṇarum puḷḷiyiṟutikaḷ, yakaramum uyirum varuvaḻi iyaṟkai - yakaramum uyirum varumoḻiyāy varumiṭattu av vukaram peṟātu iyalpāy muṭiyum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uriñ yāṉā, uriñ aṉantā; porun yāṉā, porun aṉantā; uriñ ātā, porun ātā eṉa oṭṭuka.	(21)
aḷavu niṟai eṇṇuppeyarkaḷ tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu
165.	uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟuti yāki
	aḷavum niṟaiyum eṇṇuñ cuṭṭi
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa ellāc collum
	tattaṅ kiḷavi tammakap paṭṭa
	muttai varūuṅ kālan tōṉṟiṉ
	otta teṉpa ēyeṉ cāriyai.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarum niṟaippeyarum eṇṇuppeyarum tammiṟ puṇarumāṟu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāki aḷavum niṟaiyum eṇṇum cuṭṭi uḷa eṉappaṭṭa ellāc collum - uyirum puḷḷiyum īṟāy aḷavaiyum niṟaiyaiyum eṇṇaiyum karuti uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭa ellāc coṟkaḷum, tam tam kiḷavi tam akappaṭṭa muttaivarum kālam tōṉṟiṉ ē eṉ cāriyai ottatu eṉpa - tamakku iṉamākiya coṟkaḷāyt tamakku akappaṭum moḻiyāyuḷḷaṉa tam muṉṉē varum kālam tōṉṟumāyiṉ ē eṉṉuñ cāriyai peṟṟu muṭitalaip poruntiṟṟu eṉpa e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkēyāḻākku, kalaṉēpatakku eṉa ivai aḷavup peyar; toṭiyēkaḵcu, koḷḷēaiyavi eṉa ivai niṟaip peyar; kāṇiyēmuntirikai, kālēkāṇi eṉa ivai eṇṇup peyar.
'uyirīṟu puḷḷiyīṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉē, ē eṉ cāriyai peṟātaṉavum uḷaveṉṟu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	kuṟuṇi nāṉāḻi eṉa varum.	(22)
ṉaaraiṉa varuvaḻi, mēlai ēkāraccāriyai peṟāmai
166.	araiyeṉa varūum pālvarai kiḷavikkup
	puraiva taṉṟāl cāriyai yiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) arai eṉa varum pālvarai kiḷavikkup puraivatu aṉṟu cāriyai iyaṟkai - arai eṉṟu colla varukiṉṟa poruṭkūṟṟai yuṇara niṉṟa colliṟkup poruntuvataṉṟu mēṟkūṟiya ē eṉ cāriyai peṟum iyalpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkarai, toṭiyarai, oṉṟarai eṉa varum.
itu 'oṭṭutaṟ koḻukiya vaḻakku' (puṇar. 30) aṉṟu eṉpataṉāṉ vilakkuṇṇātōveṉil, 'tammakappaṭṭa' eṉṟu varumoḻiyaiyum varaintōtiṉamaiyiṉ itaṟku av viti cellā teṉpatu.	(23)
ṉakuṟaiṉa varuvaḻi, vēṟṟumaippuṇarcci muṭipu peṟutal
167.	kuṟaiyeṉ kiḷavi muṉvaru kālai
	niṟaiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumai yiyaṟkai.
iḵtu, eytiyatu vilakkip piṟituviti uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟai eṉ kiḷavi muṉ varu kālai - kuṟai eṉṉum col aḷavu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉmuṉ varuṅkālattu, niṟaiyat tōṉṟum vēṟṟumai iyaṟkai - niṟaiyat tōṉṟum mēl (236) vēṟṟumaip poruṭpuṇarcci muṭipiṟkuc collum iyalpu e-ṟu.
e-ṭu: urikkuṟai, kalakkuṟai; toṭikkuṟai, koṭkuṟai; kāṇikkuṟai, kāṟkuṟai eṉa varum.
'muṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ, poruṭpeyaroṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum vēṟṟumai muṭipu eytukaveṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	kalappayaṟu eṉa varum.
'niṟaiya' eṉpataṉāṉ, kūṟu eṉpataṉkaṇṇum vēṟṟumai muṭipu eytumeṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	nāḻikkūṟu eṉa varum.	(24)
kuṟṟiyalukara īṟu iṉcāriyai peṟutal
168.	kuṟṟiya lukarak kiṉṉē cāriyai.
itu, vēṟṟumai muṭipu vilakki iṉ vakuttamaiyiṉ eytiyatu vilakkip piṟitu viti kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kuṟṟiyalukarakku - kuṟṟukaravīṟṟu aḷavup peyar mutaliya vaṟṟiṟku, cāriyai iṉ - kuṟaiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi varum cāriyai iṉ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	uḻakkiṉkuṟai, kaḻañciṉkuṟai, oṉṟiṉkuṟai eṉa varum.	(25)
ṉakalamṉa attuccāriyai peṟutal
169.	attiṭai varūuṅ kalameṉ aḷavē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) kalam eṉ aḷavu iṭai attu varum - kalam eṉṉum aḷavup peyar (kuṟaiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻi) iṭaiyil attu varum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalattukkuṟai eṉa varum.
itu, 'kalaṉ' eṉṉum ṉakaravīṟēl, nilaimoḻi oṟṟukkēṭum, varumoḻi oṟṟuppēṟum 'attē vaṟṟē' (puṇar. 31) eṉpataṉāṟ koḷḷappaṭum.
'kalaṉeṉ aḷavu' eṉa ōtātatu ceyyuḷiṉpam nōkkip pōlum.
cāriyai muṟkūṟiyavataṉāṉ, iṉcāriyai peṟṟavaḻi muṉ māṭṭēṟṟāṉ (167) eytiya valleḻuttu vīḻkka.	(26)
ṉapaṉaiṉayum ṉakāṉavum iṉcāriyai peṟutal
170.	paṉaiyeṉ aḷavuṅ kāveṉ niṟaiyum
	niṉaiyuṅ kālai iṉṉoṭu civaṇum.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) paṉai eṉ aḷavum kā eṉ niṟaiyum - paṉai eṉṉum aḷavup peyarum kā eṉṉum niṟaip peyarum, niṉaiyum kālai iṉṉoṭu civaṇum - (kuṟai eṉpataṉoṭu puṇarumiṭattu) ārāyuṅ kālattu iṉcāriyaiyoṭu poruntum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	paṉaiyiṉ kuṟai, kāviṉkuṟai eṉa varum.
'niṉayuṅ kālai' eṉṟataṉāṉ, valleḻuttup pēṟum ciṟu pāṉmai koḷka. (eḻut. 167)
e-ṭu:	paṉaikkuṟai, kākkuṟai eṉa varum.	(27)
aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟaippeyarkkum mutalām eḻuttu varaiyaṟai
171.	aḷaviṟkum niṟaiyiṟkum moḻimuta lāki
	uḷaveṉap paṭṭa  oṉpatiṟ ṟeḻuttē
	avaitām
	kacatapa eṉṟā namava eṉṟā
	akara ukaramō ṭavaiyeṉa moḻipa.
iḵtu, aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟaippeyarkkum mutalām eḻuttukku varaiyaṟai kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) aḷaviṟkum niṟaiyiṟkum moḻimutalāki uḷa eṉappaṭṭa oṉpatiṟṟu eḻuttu - aḷavuppeyarkkum niṟaippeyarkkum moḻi mutalāy uḷaveṉṟu collappaṭṭaṉa oṉpateḻuttu, avaitām- avai (yāvaiyōveṉiṉ), ka ca ta pa eṉṟā na ma va eṉṟā akaramōṭu ukaramōṭu avai eṉa moḻipa - ka ca ta pa kkaḷum na ma va kkaḷum akaramum ukaramumākiya avai eṉṟu colluvar e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	kalam, cāṭi, tūtai, pāṉai, nāḻi, maṇṭai, vaṭṭi, akal, uḻakku eṉavum; kaḻañcu, cīrakam, toṭi, palam, niṟai, mā, varai, antai eṉavum varum.
'uḷaveṉap paṭṭa' eṉṟataṉāṉ, uḷaveṉap paṭātaṉavum aḷavai uḷaveṉpatu.
e-ṭu:	immi, ōraṭai, ōreṭai eṉa varum.
maṟṟu iv varaiyaṟai kūṟip payantatu eṉṉai yeṉiṉ, mēl akattōttiṉuḷ (390) avaṟṟiṟku muṭipu kūṟum vaḻi, atikārattāṉ vaṉkaṇattiṉmēṟ cellātu, oḻinta kaṇattiṉum cēṟaṟku eṉpatu. (28)
ivviyaṟkup puṟaṉaṭai
172.	īṟiyal maruṅkiṉ ivaiivaṟ ṟiyalpeṉak
	kūṟiya kiḷavip pallā ṟellām
	meyttalaip paṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi
	ottavai uriya puṇarmoḻi nilaiyē.
iḵtu, iv vōttup puṟaṉaṭai.
(i-ḷ.) īṟuiyal maruṅkiṉ - uyirum puḷḷiyum iṟutiyāṉavai varumoḻiyoṭu kūṭi naṭakkumiṭattu, ivai ivaṟṟu iyalpu eṉa kūṟiya kiḷavi pal āṟu ellām - im moḻikaḷiṉ muṭipu ivaiyeṉak kūṟi muṭikkappaṭṭa coṟkaḷiṉ (avvāṟṟāṉ muṭiyātu niṉṟa) palavakai muṭipukaḷellām, mey talaippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu civaṇi ottavai uriya puṇarmoḻinilai - uṇmaiyait talaippaṭṭa vaḻakkoṭu kūṭip poruntiṉavai uriyavām puṇarum moḻikaḷ nilaimaikkaṇ e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	viḷa ñāṉṟatu eṉpuḻi nilaimoḻip peyaratu iyalpum, 'ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟu' (puṟam. 82) eṉa nilai moḻi viṉaiyāya vaḻi irumoḻi iyalpumākiya 'ña na ma ya va' (tokai marapu 2) eṉpataṉ oḻipum,
	maṇ kaṭitu eṉpuḻi varumoḻi vaṉkaṇattu iyalpākiya 'moḻimuta lākum' (tokai marapu 5) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, naṭañeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu irumoḻi iyalpum, nilkoṟṟā, niṟkoṟṟā eṉa nilaimoḻi tirinta uṟaḻcciyum, tukkoṟṟā, tuññeḷḷā eṉṉum mikutiyum, uriñuñeḷḷā eṉṉum iyalpukaṇattu ukarap pēṟum, uriñyāṉā, uriñ aṉantā eṉṉum irumoḻi iyalpum, maṇṇukoṟṟā, maṇṇuk koṟṟā, ñeḷḷā eṉavarum 'auveṉa varūum' (tokai. 10) eṉpataṉuḷ vilakkap paṭāta ṇakara ṉakara lakara ḷakara mākiya puḷḷiyiṟuti kaḷiṉ nilaimoḻi ukarap pēṟumākiya "uyirī ṟākiya muṉṉilaik kiḷavi" (tokai. 9) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, viravuppeyart tiripiṉ mēl eṭuttu ōtappaṭātaṉavāya niṉñāṇ eṉṟāṟpōla varum.
'aḵṟiṇai viravuppeyar' (tokai. 13) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, kāvikkaṇ, kuvaḷaikkaṇ eṉṟāṟpōla alvaḻi muṭipākiya 'vēṟṟumai yalvaḻi' (tokai. 13) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, patakku nāṉāḻi, cīrakarai, orumāvarai eṉṉum "uyirum puḷḷiyu miṟuti yāki" (tokai. 22) eṉpataṉ oḻipum, piṟavaṟṟiṉ oḻipumellām īṇṭē koḷka.(29)
yāvar, yātu - eṉpavaṟṟatu marūumuṭipu
173.	palaraṟi coṉmuṉ yāva reṉṉum
	peyariṭai vakaraṅ keṭutalum ēṉai
	oṉṟaṟi coṉmuṉ yāteṉ viṉāviṭai
	oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum iraṇṭum
	maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ tiriyumaṉ payiṉṟē.
itu, marūu muṭipu kūṟutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) palar aṟicolmuṉ yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭai vakaram keṭutalum - palarai aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varukiṉṟa yāvar eṉṉum peyariṭaiyil vakaram keṭutalum, ēṉai oṉṟu aṟi colmuṉ yātu eṉ viṉā iṭai oṉṟiya vakaram varutalum - oḻinta oṉṟaṉai aṟiyum colmuṉṉar varum yātu eṉṉum viṉāmoḻiyiṭai uyiroṭu poruntiya vakaram varutalum, iraṇṭum maruviṉ pāttiyiṉ payiṉṟu tiriyum - iraṇṭum marūukkaḷatu muṭipiṉiṭattup payiṉṟu vaḻaṅkum e-ṟu.
e-ṭu:	avar yār eṉavum, atu yāvatu eṉavum varum.
'oṉṟiya' eṉṟataṉāṉ, vakara uyirmey eṉṟu koḷka. iṉṉum ataṉāṉē, yāreṉpatum yāvateṉpatum nilaimoḻiyāyp piṟa varumoḻiyoṭu puṇarumvaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷka.
e-ṭu:	'yāryārk kaṇṭē yuvappar' eṉavum, "yāvatu naṉṟeṉa vuṇarār māṭṭum" (kuṟun. 78) eṉavum varum.
'payiṉṟu' eṉṟataṉāṉ, palaraṟi collum oṉṟaṟi collum varumoḻiyāya vaḻiyum im muṭipu koḷḷappaṭum.
e-ṭu: yāravar, yāvatatu eṉa varum.	(30)
